###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 2:5:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/2:5:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/2:5:8)


**מִפְּנֵי שֶׁלֹּא נִגְמְרָה מְלַאכְתָּן.** דַּחֲתִיכָה שֶׁחוֹתְכִין אוֹתָן בְּאֶמְצָעָן זוֹ הִיא גְּמַר מְלַאכְתָּן. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי אֶלְעָזָר בַּר צָדוֹק. אֶלָּא לִבּוּנָן בַּכִּבְשָׁן הוּא גְּמַר מְלַאכְתָּן, הִלְכָּךְ מִטַּמְּאוֹת בְּמַשָּׂא הַזָּב אֲפִלּוּ קֹדֶם שֶׁחִלְּקָן:


מפני שלא נגמרה מלאכתן – for the cutting that is made in their middle, this is the completion of their work [of creation]. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Eleazar ben Zadok, but glazing in a furnace is the completion of their work, therefore they become defiled through contact with the person who has a flux even before they are divided.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 2:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/2:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/2:6:1)


**מְלִילוֹת.** שִׁבֳּלִים שֶׁלֹּא בָּשְׁלוּ כָּל צָרְכָּן, וְטוֹעֲנָן בַּאֲבָנִים וּמַשְׁקֶה זָב מֵהֶן וְטוֹבֵל בּוֹ:


מלילות – ears of corn that did not ripen completely, and they load them with stones and the liquid flows from them and he immerses in it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 2:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/2:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/2:6:2)


**יִגְמֹר.** מֵאַחַר שֶׁרִסְּקָן וְכִתְּתָן מֵעֶרֶב שַׁבָּת, יִגְמֹר וְיֹאכַל בְּשַׁבָּת. וְלֹא דָמִי לְמַשְׁקִים שֶׁזָּבוּ שֶׁאֲסוּרִין גְּזֵרָה שֶׁמָּא יִסְחֹט, דְּהָכָא אֲפִלּוּ יִסְחֹט לֵיכָּא אִסּוּרָא דְאוֹרַיְתָא, מִשּׁוּם דְּמַשְׁקִים אֲתוּ מִמֵּילָא:


יגמור – for after they have been pressed and crushed from Friday, he completes and may eat [them] on Shabbat. And it is not similar to liquids that flowed that are forbidden, as a decree lest he wring out [the liquid], for here, even if he should wring out [the liquids], there is no prohibition from the Torah, because the liquids come of their own accord.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 2:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/2:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/2:6:3)


**לֹא יִגְמֹר.** כְּדֵי לֶאֱכֹל לְאַחַר שֶׁקִּדַּשׁ הַיּוֹם, דַּאֲסוּרִין כִּשְׁאָר מַשְׁקִים שֶׁזָּבוּ. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יִשְׁמָעֵאל:


לא יגמור – in order to eat after he has sanctified the [Sabbath] day, for it is prohibited like other liquids that flowed. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yishmael.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 2:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/2:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/2:7:1)


**עִיר שֶׁל זָהָב.** עֲטֶרֶת זָהָב עֲשׂוּיָה כְּמִין עִיר צוּרַת יְרוּשָׁלַיִם. וְלֹא חָיְשִׁינַן דִּלְמָא שָׁלְפָא וּמְחַוְּיָא וְאָתְיָא לַאֲתוֹיֵי אַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים:


עיר של זהב – golden crown that is made like the form of the city of Jerusalem, and we don’t suspect that perhaps she will take it off and show it and will bring it four cubits in the public domain (versus Tractate Shabbat, Chapter 6, Mishnah 1, which teaches in an anonymous Mishnah that a woman should not go out on Shabbat with a golden crown that is made like the form of the city of Jerusalem).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 2:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/2:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/2:7:2)


**וּמַפְרִיחֵי יוֹנִים.** מִין מִמִּינֵי הַשְּׂחוֹק. אִם תַּקְדִּים יוֹנָתְךָ לְיוֹנָתִי אֶתֵּן לְךָ כָּךְ וְכָךְ. פֵּרוּשׁ אַחֵר, שֶׁמְּגַדֵּל יוֹנָה מְלֻמֶּדֶת לְהָבִיא יוֹנִים לַבַּיִת בַּעֲלֶיהָ, וְיֵשׁ בָּהֶן גָּזֵל מִשּׁוּם דַּרְכֵי שָׁלוֹם וְלֹא גָּזֵל גָּמוּר:


ומפריחי יונים – one of the kinds of sport/jest [that one plays with one’s pigeons]: if your pigeon will come before my pigeon, I will give you such-and-such, for one who raises a pigeon who is knowledgeable to bring [other] pigeons to the house in the upper story. And there is through them theft because of the ways of peace, but not complete theft.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 2:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/2:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/2:7:3)


**סָפֵק נָגַע סָפֵק לֹא נָגַע סְפֵקוֹ טָהוֹר.** מִשּׁוּם דְּטֻמְאָה עוֹבֶרֶת הִיא וְאֵינָהּ נָחָה בַּמָּקוֹם, הִלְכָּךְ סְפֵקוֹ טָהוֹר וַאֲפִלּוּ בִּרְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד:


ספק נגע ספק לא נגע ספיקו טהור – because it is a passing defilement and does not rest in a place, therefore, its doubt is ritually pure and even in the private domain.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 2:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/2:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/2:8:1)


**סַנְדָּל שֶׁל סַיָּדִין.** מִנְעָל שֶׁל עֵץ שֶׁנּוֹעֲלִין בְּרַגְלֵיהֶם הַמִּתְעַסְּקִים בַּסִּיד לְהָגֵן עַל רַגְלֵיהֶם שֶׁלֹּא יִשָּׂרְפוּ בַּסִּיד. וְאִם נָעַל אוֹתוֹ הַזָּב, טָמֵא מִדְרָס:


סנדל של סיידין – the wooden shoe that they wear on their feat when they are engaged with lime/plaster to protect their feet that they don’t burn in the lime, and if a person with a flux wore it, it would be impure through Levitical uncleanness arising from a someone with a flux’s immediate contact by treading [or leaning against].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 2:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/2:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/2:8:2)


**וְעַל שְׁיָרֵי תַנּוּר אַרְבָּעָה.** אִם הָיָה גָּדוֹל הַרְבֵּה וְנִטְמָא, וְאַחַר כָּךְ נִשְׁבַּר, לֹא טָהֵר עַד שֶׁלֹּא יִהְיֶה בִּשְׁבָרָיו גָּבוֹהַּ אַרְבָּעָה, וּלְעוֹלָם שְׁבָרָיו מְקַבְּלִים טֻמְאָה כְּאִלּוּ הָיָה שָׁלֵם עַד שֶׁיִּהְיֶה הַשֶּׁבֶר פָּחוֹת מִגֹּבַהּ אַרְבָּעָה. וּסְתָם תַּנּוּר שֶׁבַּמִּשְׁנָה עָשׂוּי כְּמִין קְדֵרָה גְּדוֹלָה שֶׁאֵין לָהּ שׁוּלַיִם וּמְחַבְּרִין אוֹתוֹ בְּטִיט עַל גַּבֵּי קַרְקַע וְרִצְפַּת הַקַּרְקַע הִיא תַּחְתִּיתוֹ שֶׁל תַּנּוּר:


ועל שיירי תנור ארבעה – if it was large enough and it became defiled and afterwards broke, he would not be pure until there would not be in its shards a height of four [handbreadths], for shards are susceptible to receiving defilement as if it was whole, until the shard would be less than the height of three [handbreadths]. But a mere oven that is in the Mishnah is made like a large pot which has no rim and when they fasten it with plaster on the ground and the floor of the ground is the bottom of the oven.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 2:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/2:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/2:8:3)


**שְׁנַיִם מֵחִפּוּיָיו.** מִן הַלּוּחוֹת הָעֲשׂוּיוֹת לְמוֹשָׁב:


שנים מחיפוייו – from the boards that are made for sitting.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 2:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/2:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/2:8:4)


**שֶׁרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא מְטַמֵּא.** דְּסָבַר אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵינוֹ רָאוּי לִישִׁיבָה הוּא רָאוּי לְקַבֵּל רִמּוֹנִים, וְטָמֵא מִשּׁוּם כְּלִי קִבּוּל:


שרבי עקיבא מטמא – as he {i.e., Rabbi Akiva) holds that even though it is not appropriate for sitting, it is appropriate to receive pomegranates, and it is impure because it is a receptacle.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 2:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/2:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/2:8:5)


**וַחֲכָמִים מְטַהֲרִין.** דְּסָבְרֵי הוֹאִיל וְעִקָּרוֹ לִישִׁיבָה עֲבִיד וְלֹא לְבֵית קִבּוּל, כֵּיוָן שֶׁנִּתְבַּטֵּל הָעִקָּר שֶׁבַּעֲבוּרוֹ נַעֲשָׂה, נִתְבַּטֵּל הַטָּפֵל, וְאֵינוֹ מִטַּמֵּא אֲפִלּוּ מִשּׁוּם כְּלִי קִבּוּל. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


וחכמים מטהרין – as they hold because its essential purpose is that it was made for sitting and not as a receptacle, since the purpose for which it had been made was annulled. And it does not defile even because of being a receptacle. And the Halakha is according to the Sages.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 2:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/2:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/2:9:1)


**הָאָב זוֹכֶה לַבֵּן וְכוּ'.** לְפִי שֶׁקָּרוֹב טֶבַע הַבֵּן לִהְיוֹת דּוֹמֶה לְטֶבַע הָאָב, וְהַנּוֹי וְהַכֹּחַ וְהַחָכְמָה וְהַשָּׁנִים אָדָם זוֹכֶה בָּהֶן מִתְּחִלַּת יְצִירָתוֹ כְּפִי טִבְעוֹ, וְהָעֹשֶׁר אָדָם מוֹרִישׁוֹ לִבְנוֹ. וְחָכְמָה דְאָמְרִינַן הָכָא, הִיא הַסְּבָרָא הַיְשָׁרָה, וְהִיא בָּאָדָם כְּפִי טִבְעוֹ. וְהַשָּׁנִים, אֲרִיכוּת הַיָּמִים. וְלִי נִרְאֶה, שֶׁאִם אָדָם זוֹכֶה הָוְיָן לֵיהּ בָּנִים נָאִים בַּעֲלֵי כֹּחַ בַּעֲלֵי חָכְמָה עֲשִׁירִים וּמַאֲרִיכֵי יָמִים:


האב זוכה לבן וכו' – since it is close that the nature of the son is to be similar to the nature of the father, and beauty, and strength and wisdom and years, a man endows [his son] with them from the beginning of his creation according to his nature, and wealth a person bequeaths to his son. And wisdom that we speak of here, is the straight ingenuity of mind/brightness of mind, which is in a person according to his nature. And years, length of days. And to me it appears that if a person endows them, he will have sons who are pleasant, strong, wise, rich and who live long lives.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 2:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/2:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/2:9:2)


**וּבְמִסְפַּר הַדּוֹרוֹת לְפָנָיו.** פְּעָמִים שֶׁהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא מַבְטִיחַ לָאָב לַעֲשׂוֹת טוֹבָה לְזַרְעוֹ לְדוֹר שְׁלִישִׁי אוֹ לְדוֹר רְבִיעִי, וְהָאָב זָכָה לְאוֹתוֹ הַדּוֹר שֶׁתָּבֹא לָהֶם אוֹתָהּ הַטּוֹבָה:


ובמספר הדורות לפניו – Sometimes that the Holy One, blessed be He promises that his father will do good for his seed to the third generation or to the fourth generation, and the father endowed to that generation that this particular goodness will come to them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 2:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/2:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/2:9:3)


**הוּא הַקֵּץ.** שֶׁקָּצַב הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא לְאוֹתוֹ דָּבָר שֶׁיִּהְיֶה לִזְמַן פְּלוֹנִי וּלְדוֹר פְּלוֹנִי. כְּמוֹ וַעֲבָדוּם וְעִנּוּ אֹתָם אַרְבַּע מֵאוֹת שָׁנָה, וְדוֹר רְבִיעִי יָשׁוּבוּ הֵנָּה, הֲרֵי מִסְפַּר הַדּוֹרוֹת שֶׁהוּא דּוֹר רְבִיעִי הוּא הַקֵּץ שֶׁל אַרְבַּע מֵאוֹת שָׁנָה:


הוא הקץ – that the Holy One, blessed be He, determined for this matter that it will be for a specific time and for a certain generation, such as (Genesis 15:14,16): “…and they shall be enslaved and oppressed four hundred years…and they shall return here in the fourth generation…,” that the number of generations which is the fourth generation will be end of the four hundred years.



### Mishnah 10

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 2:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/2:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/2:10:1)


**שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר וְהָיָה מִדֵּי חֹדֶשׁ בְּחָדְשׁוֹ.** כְּשֶׁיָּבֹא חֹדֶשׁ בְּאוֹתוֹ הַחֹדֶשׁ שֶׁמֵּת בּוֹ, יֵצֵא מִגֵּיהִנֹּם וְיָבֹא לְהִשְׁתַּחֲווֹת לִפְנֵי ה':


שנאמר והיה מדי חודש בחדשו – when the month comes in the same month that he died in it, he would leave from Gehinnom and come to worship before God.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 2:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/2:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/2:10:2)


**שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר וּמִדֵּי שַׁבָּת בְּשַׁבַּתּוֹ.** כְּלוֹמַר לְאַחַר שֶׁיִּהְיֶה בַּגֵּיהִנֹּם כָּל כָּךְ יָמִים כְּמוֹ שֶׁיֵּשׁ מִיּוֹם רִאשׁוֹן שֶׁל פֶּסַח דְּאִיקְרֵי שַׁבָּת דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא כג) וּסְפַרְתֶּם לָכֶם מִמָּחֳרַת הַשַּׁבָּת וְגוֹ'‏ עַד עֲצֶרֶת שֶׁהוּא מִמָּחֳרַת הַשַּׁבָּת הַשְּׁבִיעִית יֵצֵא מִגֵּיהִנֹּם וְיָבֹא לְהִשְׁתַּחֲווֹת:


שנאמר ומדי שבת בשבתו – that is to say, after he would be in Gehinnom so many days like there are from the first day of Passover, which is called “Shabbat,” as it is written (Leviticus 23:15): “[And from the day on which you bring the sheaf of elevation offering]– the day after the Sabbath – you shall count off seven weeks. [They must be complete],” until Atzeret/Shavuot which is the from the morrow of the seventh Sabbath, he left from Gehinnom and comes to worship.



## Chapter 3


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 3:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:1:1)


**כָּל הַמְטַמְּאִין בְּאֹהֶל.** כְּגוֹן הַמֵּת וְהַנֶּצֶל וּמְלֹא תַּרְוַד רָקָב, וְכָל אוֹתָן הַשְּׁנוּיִין רֵישׁ פֶּרֶק ב'‏ דְּאֳהָלוֹת:


כל המטמאין באוהל – such as the dead, and the decayed matter (i..e., liquid and coagulated portions of a corpse – see Tractate Ohalot, Chapter 2, Mishnah 1) and a spoonful of dust [of earth from a grave of decaying human body], and all those that are taught at the beginning of the second chapter of Tractate Ohalot.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 3:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:1:2)


**שֶׁנֶּחְלְקוּ.** שִׁעוּרֵיהֶן לַחֲצָאִים:


שנחלקו – their measures in halves.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 3:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:1:3)


**וְהִכְנִיסָן.** כְּשֶׁהֵן מְחֻלָּקִין:


שהכניסן – when they are divided.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 3:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:1:4)


**לְתוֹךְ הַבַּיִת.** דְּהַיְנוּ לְתוֹךְ אֹהֶל אֶחָד:


לתוך הבית – that is inside one tent.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 3:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:1:5)


**רַבִּי דוֹסָא בֶּן הַרְכִּינָס מְטַהֵר.** דְּסָבַר לֹא מִצְטָרְפֵי לִכְשִׁעוּר:


רבי דוסא בן הרכינס מטהר – that he holds that they do not combine to the appropriate measurement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 3:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:1:6)


**הַנּוֹגֵעַ בְּכִשְׁנֵי חֲצָאֵי זֵיתִים מִן הַנְּבֵלָה.** פָּתַח בְּאֹהֶל וּמְפָרֵשׁ נְבֵלָה, לוֹמַר לְךָ דְּכִי הֵיכִי דִסְבִירָא לֵיהּ לְרַבִּי דוֹסָא שֶׁאֵין מִצְטָרְפִין לְטֻמְאַת אֹהֶל, הָכִי נַמִּי סְבִירָא לֵיהּ שֶׁאֵין מִצְטָרְפִין טֻמְאַת מַגָּע וּמַשָּׂא, בֵּין בְּמֵת בֵּין בִּנְבֵלָה:


הנוגע בכשני חצאי זיתים מן הנבילה – he started about tent impurity and continued with impurity of a carrion, to tell you that just as Rabbi Dosa holds that they do not combine for the defilement of a tent, so too, he holds that they defilement of contact and carrying do not combine, whether with the dead person or a carrion.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 3:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:1:7)


**הַנּוֹגֵעַ בַּחֲצִי זַיִת וּמַאֲהִיל עַל חֲצִי זַיִת.** שֶׁהַמַּאֲהִיל עַל הַמֵּת טָמֵא כְּאִלּוּ הָיָה עִם הַמֵּת בָּאֹהֶל. וְכָל הָנָךְ מַאֲהִיל דְּרֵישָׁא, בְּטֻמְאָה רְצוּצָה אַיְרֵי, שֶׁאֵין בֵּין הַטֻּמְאָה וּבֵין הָאֹהֶל פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח, וּמִשּׁוּם הָכִי חֲכָמִים מְטַמְּאִים, דְּאֹהֶל כִּי הַאי, נְגִיעָה הִיא. וְסֵיפָא דִתְנַן אֲבָל הַנּוֹגֵעַ בְּכַחֲצִי זַיִת וְדָבָר אַחֵר מַאֲהִיל עָלָיו וְכוּ'‏ דְּמוֹדוּ רַבָּנָן דְּטָהוֹר, מַיְרֵי כְּשֶׁיֵּשׁ פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח בֵּין הַטֻּמְאָה וּבֵין הָאֹהֶל, דִּבְהָא מוֹדוּ רַבָּנָן דְּאֵין מִצְטָרְפִין, דִּנְגִיעָה וְאֹהֶל שְׁנֵי שֵׁמוֹת הֵן, וְכָל שֶׁהֵן מִשְּׁנֵי שֵׁמוֹת אֵין מִצְטָרְפִין. וְרַבִּי דוֹסָא סָבַר דַּאֲפִלּוּ בְּטֻמְאָה רְצוּצָה אֵין מִצְטָרְפִין:


הנוגע בחצי זית ומאהיל על חצי זית – When one overshadows on a dead person, he is impure as if he had been with the dead person in the tent. And all of these where he is overshadowing in the first part of the Mishnah, we are speaking of squeezed defilement, for there is no difference between the defilement and the tent that is open a hand-breadth, and because of this, the Sages defile, for a tent like this, it is contact, and at the end of the Mishnah it is taught that if one is in contact with half-olive’s bulk and another thing, it overshadows upon him, etc., and the Rabbis admit that he is pure, when speaking where there is a hand-breadth’s opening whether between the defilement and the tent. And in this, the Rabbis admit that they don’t combine, for contact and tent are two different modes of contamination and whatever is from two different modes does not combine. But Rabbi Dosa holds that even with pressed defilement, they don’t combine.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 3:1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:1:8)


**אָמַר רַבִּי מֵאִיר וְכוּ'.** רַבִּי מֵאִיר פָּלֵיג אַתַּנָּא קַמָּא דְּאָמַר דִּכְשֶׁיֵּשׁ פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח בֵּין הַטֻּמְאָה וְהָאֹהֶל מוֹדוּ רַבָּנָן דְּטָהוֹר וְאֵין מִצְטָרְפִין, וְאָמַר אִיהוּ דְּאַף בְּזוֹ פְּלִיגֵי רַבָּנָן עַל רַבִּי דוֹסָא וְאָמְרֵי דִּנְגִיעָה וְאֹהֶל חַד שְׁמָא הוּא, הִלְכָּךְ מִצְטָרְפִין לִכְשִׁעוּר וּמְטַמְּאִין. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי מֵאִיר:


רבי מאיר וכו' – Rabbi Meir disputes the first Tanna who said that when there is a hand-breadth’s opening between the defilement and the tent, the Rabbis admit that he is pure, and it doesn’t combine. And he said that even in this, the Rabbis dispute on Rabbi Dosa and hold that contact and the tent category. Therefore, they combine regarding the measurement and defile, but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Meir.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 3:1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:1:9)


**חוּץ מִן הַמַּגָּע עִם הַמַּשָּׂא.** כְּגוֹן שֶׁנָּגַע בַּחֲצִי זַיִת וְהֵסִיט חֲצִי זַיִת:


חוץ מן המגע עם המשא – such as the case where he came in contact with one-half an olive’s bulk and shook one-half an olive’s bulk.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 3:1:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:1:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:1:10)


**וְהַמַּשָּׂא עִם הָאֹהֶל.** שֶׁהֵסִיט חֲצִי זַיִת וְהֶאֱהִיל עַל חֲצִי זַיִת:


והמשא עם האוהל – that he shook one-half an olive’s bulk and overshadowed on the other one-half.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 3:1:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:1:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:1:11)


**מִשֵּׁם אֶחָד.** כְּגוֹן מַגָּע וּמַגָּע, מַשָּׂא וּמַשָּׂא, אֹהֶל וְאֹהֶל:


משם אחד – such one type of contact and another kind, one kind of carrying and another, one tent and another tent.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 3:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:2:1)


**אֹכֶל פָּרוּד.** כְּגוֹן צִבּוּר שֶׁל אֱגוֹזִים בָּטְנִים וּשְׁקֵדִים, שֶׁכְּשֶׁהֵן נִפְרָדִים זֶה מִזֶּה אֵין בָּהֶם כַּבֵּיצָה שֶׁהוּא שִׁעוּר טֻמְאַת אֳכָלִים לְטַמֵּא אֲחֵרִים:


אוכל פרוד – such as a pile of nuts, peanuts and almonds, when they are separated one from the other, they lack the equivalent of an egg which is the measure for defilment of foods to defile others.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 3:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:2:2)


**אֵינוֹ מִצְטָרֵף.** וְאִם הֵן טְמֵאִים וְנָגְעוּ בָּהֶן טָהֳרוֹת כְּשֶׁהֵן צְבוּרִין אֵינָן מִצְטָרְפִין זֶה עִם זֶה לְטַמֵּא הַטָּהֳרוֹת, כֵּיוָן שֶׁאֵין בְּכָל אֶחָד מֵהֶן בִּפְנֵי עַצְמוֹ כְּשִׁעוּר טֻמְאַת אֳכָלִין:


אינו מצטרף – and if they were ritually impure and came in contact with ritually pure [foods] when they are in a pile, they do not combine with each other to defile the pure [foods] since there isn’t among any of them on its own like the measure for the defiling of foods.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 3:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:2:3)


**אֲסִימוֹן.** עָשׂוּי כְּמִדַּת הַמַּטְבֵּעַ הַיּוֹצֵא, אֶלָּא שֶׁעֲדַיִן לֹא טָבְעוּ עָלָיו צוּרָה. וְאֵין פּוֹדִין בּוֹ מַעֲשֵׂר שֵׁנִי, דִּכְתִיב (דברים יד) וְצַרְתָּ הַכֶּסֶף בְּיָדְךָ, דָּבָר שֶׁיֵּשׁ עָלָיו צוּרָה:


אסימון – it is made like the form of the coin in vogue, but they had not yet formulated the coin (i.e., a picture indicating the value), and we don’t redeem Second Tithe with it, as it is written (Deuteronomy 14:25): “Wrap up the money n your hand,” something that has upon it a form.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 3:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:2:4)


**מַטְבִּילִין יָדַיִם לַחַטָּאת.** מִי שֶׁנָּגַע בָּאֳכָלִין וּמַשְׁקִין טְמֵאִים וְכַיּוֹצֵא בָּהֶן מִדְּבָרִים שֶׁמְּטַמְּאִין אֶת הַיָּדַיִם וְאֵין מְטַמְּאִין כָּל הַגּוּף, הֲרֵי זֶה מַטְבִּיל יָדָיו בִּלְבַד, וְכָשֵׁר לִגַּע בְּמֵי חַטָּאת וּלְהַזּוֹת מֵאֵפֶר הַפָּרָה עַל הַטָּמֵא:


מטבילין ידים לחטאת – Whomever has come in contact with impure food-stuffs and liquids and similar thing from those things that defile the hands and don’t defile the entire body, he immerses his hands only, and it is fit to com in contact with the waters of purification (i.e., water mixed with the ashes of the Red Heifer) and to be sprinkled from the ashes of the [Red] Heifer on the impurity.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 3:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:2:5)


**אִם נִטְמְאוּ יָדָיו נִטְמָא גוּפוֹ.** וְצָרִיךְ טְבִילָה לְכָל הַגּוּף, דְּמַעֲלָה עָשׂוּ בְּמֵי חַטָּאת שֶׁאִם נִטְמְאוּ יָדָיו נִטְמָא גּוּפוֹ, מַה שֶּׁאֵין כֵּן בִּתְרוּמָה וְקָדָשִׁים:


אם נטמאו ידיו נטמא גופו – and it requires immersion of the entire body, for they made a preference for the waters of purification that if his hands had been defiled, his entire body was defiled, which is not the case with Priest’s Due/Terumah and Holy Things [in the Temple].



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 3:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:3:1)


**מְעֵי אֲבַטִּיחַ.** הַזֵּרְעוֹנִים וְהַמַּשְׁקֶה שֶׁבְּתוֹךְ הָאֲבַטִּיחַ:


מעי אבטיח – the seeds and the liquid that is within the melon.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 3:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:3:2)


**וּקְנִיבַת יָרָק.** עָלִין מְעֻפָּשִׁים שֶׁמְּסִירִים מִן הַיָּרָק:


וקניבת ירק – the mouldy/decayed leaves that we remove from the vegetables.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 3:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:3:3)


**מָנֶה וּמָנֶה וּפְרָס.** כָּל אַחַת מָנֶה וָחֵצִי. וּבְצִיר מֵהָכִי לֹא חָשֵׁיב גֵּז, שֶׁזּוֹ פָּחוּת שֶׁבַּגִּזּוֹת:


מנה מנה ופרס (this segment of the Mishnah is also taught in Tractate Hullin, Chapter 11, Mishnah 2) – Each one is worth a Maneh and one-half and less than this is not considered fleece/shorn wool for this is the least of the fleece.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 3:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:3:4)


**כָּל שֶׁהֵן.** לָאו דַּוְקָא. דִּבְפָחוֹת מִשִּׁשִּׁים סְלָעִים לֹא מְחַיְּבֵי רַבָּנָן בְּרֵאשִׁית הַגֵּז. אֶלָּא מִשּׁוּם דְּרַבִּי דוֹסָא יָהֵיב שִׁעוּרָא רַבָּה, קָרֵי תַּנָּא לְשִׁעוּרָא זוּטָא דְרַבָּנָן כָּל שֶׁהֵן. וְהַסֶּלַע מִשְׁקָלוֹ עֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבָּעָה מָעִים, וּמִשְׁקַל כָּל מָעָה שֵׁשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה גַּרְעִינֵי שְׂעֹרָה:


כל שהן – not exactly for less than sixth Selah the Rabbis do not obligated in the first shorn wood (which is the priest’s gift), but because Rabbi Dosa provides a large measurement, the Tannah calls for the small measurement of the Rabbis, however much they produce. And the weight of the Sela is twenty-four Ma’im and the weight of each M’ah is sixteen globules of barley-seed.s



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 3:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:4:1)


**חוֹצָלוֹת.** מַחְצְלָאוֹת עֲשׂוּיוֹת מִגֶּמִי וְכַיּוֹצֵא בּוֹ, וְיֵשׁ לָהֶם שָׂפָה סָבִיב:


חולצות – matting that is made from bulrushes/reed-grass and things similar to it. And they have a rim surrounding it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 3:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:4:2)


**טְמֵאוֹת טְמֵא מֵת.** שֶׁהֲרֵי יֵשׁ לָהֶן בֵּית קִבּוּל. אֲבָל לֹא טֻמְאַת מִשְׁכָּב וּמוֹשָׁב, לְפִי שֶׁאֵין רְאוּיוֹת לִשְׁכִיבָה וְלֹא לִישִׁיבָה:


טמאות טמא מת – for they have a receptacle but defilement of lying and sitting, because they are not appropriate for lying nor for sitting.-


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 3:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:4:3)


**וַחֲכָמִים אוֹמְרִים מִדְרָס.** דַּחֲזוּ נַמִּי לִשְׁכִיבָה:


וחכמים אומרים מדרס – for they are also suitable to be lain upon.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 3:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:4:4)


**הַקְּלִיעוֹת.** חוּטִין מַעֲשֵׂה עֲבוֹת:


הקליעות – threats that are made in a matting/network.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 3:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:4:5)


**טְהוֹרוֹת.** שֶׁאֵינָן חֲשׁוּבוֹת אָרִיג וְאֵין זֶה בֶּגֶד:


טהורות – that are not considered woven/in a web and this is not clothing.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 3:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:4:6)


**חוּץ מִשֶּׁל גַּלְגִּילוֹן.** הַקְּלִיעוֹת שֶׁל אַבְנֵט שֶׁחוֹגְרִים בּוֹ, שֶׁבֶּגֶד הוּא חָשׁוּב:


חוץ משל גלגילון – screens/networks of belt that they gird with it, and it is considered clothing.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 3:4:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:4:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:4:7)


**כֻּלָּם טְמֵאוֹת.** דְּכֻלָּם חֲשׁוּבִים בֶּגֶד, חוּץ מִן הַקְּלִיעָה שֶׁעוֹשִׂים מוֹכְרֵי הַצֶּמֶר לִקְשֹׁר בָּהּ אֲגֻדּוֹת שֶׁל צֶמֶר:


כולם טמאות – for all are considered clothing, except for the screens that those who sell wool make to tie on it bundles of wool.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 3:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:5:1)


**קֶלַע.** שֶׁזּוֹרְקִים בּוֹ הָאֲבָנִים. כְּמָה דְאַתְּ אָמַר (שופטים כ) קֹלֵעַ בָּאֶבֶן. (שמואל א כה) יְקַלְּעֶנָּה בְּתוֹךְ כַּף הַקָּלַע:


קלע – [the sling] that they throw with it stones, like you say (Judges 20:16): “ [Of all this force- 700 picked men -were left handed.] Every one of them could sling a stone at a hair and not miss.” (1 Samuel 25:29): ”But he will fling away the lives of your enemies as from the hollow of a sling.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 3:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:5:2)


**בֵּית קִבּוּל שֶׁלָּהּ.** הַמָּקוֹם שֶׁמְּשִׂימִין בּוֹ הָאֶבֶן שֶׁרוֹצִים לְהַשְׁלִיךְ:


בית קיבול שלה – the place where they put in the stone that they want to throw.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 3:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:5:3)


**בֵּית אֶצְבַּע שֶׁלָּהּ.** כְּמִין נֶקֶב קָטָן שֶׁמַּכְנִיסִין בּוֹ הָאֶצְבַּע:


בית אצבע שלה – like a small hole that one puts one’s finger into.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 3:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:5:4)


**בֵּית הַפָּקִיעַ.** הַבֵּית יָד שֶׁאוֹחֵז בּוֹ כְּשֶׁמְּסַבֵּב הַקֶּלַע בָּאֲוִיר וְרוֹצֶה לְהַפְקִיעַ וּלְהַשְׁלִיךְ הָאֶבֶן:


בית הפקיע – the handle that one holds on to when one twirls the sling in the air to release and throw the stone.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 3:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:6:1)


**הַשְּׁבוּיָה.** אֵשֶׁת כֹּהֵן שֶׁנִּשְׁבֵּית:


שבויה – the wife of a Kohen who was taken captive (also, the daughter of a Kohen).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 3:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:6:2)


**אוֹכֶלֶת בַּתְּרוּמָה.** וְלֹא חָיְשִׁינַן שֶׁמָּא הַנָּכְרִי בָּא עָלֶיהָ וַעֲשָׂאָהּ זוֹנָה וּפְסָלָהּ מִן הַתְּרוּמָה:


אוכלת בתרומה – and we don’t suspect lest a heathen came upon her and made her a harlot nad invalidated her from the Terumah/priest’s due.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 3:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:6:3)


**שֶׁהַפֶּה שֶׁאָסַר הוּא הַפֶּה שֶׁהִתִּיר.** כְּשֵׁם שֶׁהֶאֱמִינוּהָ כְּשֶׁאָמְרָה נִשְׁבֵּיתִי, כָּךְ יֵשׁ לְהַאֲמִינָהּ כְּשֶׁאוֹמֶרֶת וּטְהוֹרָה אֲנִי:


שהפה שאסר הוא הפה שהתיר (this section of the Mishnah is taught in Tractate Ketubot, Chapter 2, Mishnah 5) – just as they believed her when she said: “I had been taken captive,” so one is able to believe her when she says, “and I am pure.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 3:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:7:1)


**הַטָּמֵא עוֹמֵד.** מְצֹרָע עוֹמֵד תַּחַת הָאֹהֶל אוֹ תַּחַת הָאִילָן:


הטמא עומד – the leper stands under the tent or under the tree.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 3:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:7:2)


**וְטָהוֹר עוֹבֵר.** דֶּרֶךְ שָׁם. סָפֵק נָגַע סָפֵק לֹא נָגַע, אוֹ סָפֵק הֶאֱהִיל הָאִילָן וְהָאֹהֶל עַל שְׁנֵיהֶם וְנִטְמָא הַטָּהוֹר, סָפֵק לֹא הֶאֱהִיל וְלֹא נִטְמָא, הֲרֵי סָפֵק אֶחָד. אוֹ שֶׁהָיָה הַטָּהוֹר עוֹמֵד וְהַמְצֹרָע עוֹבֵר, זֶה סָפֵק שֵׁנִי:


וטהור עובר – on the way there (i.e., to the tent or tree). It is doubtful if he came in contact or did not come in contact [with it] or if he was overshadowed by the tree or was overshadowed by both of them and both the pure became impure, and it is doubtful if he was not overshadowed and not defiled, this is one doubt or the pure one stood and the leper passed by, which is the second doubt.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 3:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:7:3)


**טֻמְאָה בִּרְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד וְטָהֳרָה בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים.** כְּגוֹן חֲנוּת הַפְּתוּחָה לִרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים, סָפֵק נִכְנַס סָפֵק לֹא נִכְנַס:


טומאה ברשות היחיד וטהרה ברשות הרבים – such as a store open to the public domain, it is doubtful if he entered and it is doubtful if he did not enter.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 3:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:7:4)


**אוֹ שֶׁטָּהֳרָה בִּרְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד וְהַטֻּמְאָה בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים.** הֲרֵי שְׁנֵי סְפֵקוֹת אֲחֵרוֹת:


או שטהרה ברשות היחיד והטומאה ברשות הרבים – these are two other doubtful cases.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 3:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:7:5)


**רַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ מְטַמֵּא.** דְּסָבַר הוֹאִיל וּרְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד מְשֻׁתֶּפֶת בִּסְפֵק טֻמְאָה זוֹ, הָוֵי סְפֵק טֻמְאָה בִּרְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד וּסְפֵקוֹ טָמֵא:


רבי יהושע מטמא – as he holds that since the private domain is combined/joint in the doubtfulness of this defilement, this is a doubtful defilement in the private domain and its doubtfulness is defiled.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 3:7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:7:6)


**וַחֲכָמִים מְטַהֲרִין.** דְּסָבְרֵי הוֹאִיל וּסְפֵק טֻמְאָה בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים מְשֻׁתֶּפֶת בָּהּ, הָוֵי סְפֵק טֻמְאָה בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים וּסְפֵקוֹ טָהוֹר. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ:


וחכמים מטהרים – for they hold that since a doubtful defilement in the public domain is combined in it, this is doubtful defilement in the public domain and its doubtfulness is pure. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehoshua.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 3:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:8:1)


**מַסְמֵר הַשֻּׁלְחָנִי.** שֶׁהַשֻּׁלְחָנִי תּוֹלֶה בּוֹ כַּף הַמֹּאזְנַיִם. וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ פֵּרְשׁוּ, מַסְמֵר קְבוּעָה בָּעַמּוּד שֶׁלִּפְנֵי הַשֻּׁלְחָנִי לְהַעֲמִיד בּוֹ תְּרִיסֵי הַחֲנוּת. וּמְטַהֲרִין רַבָּנָן מִשּׁוּם דְּתַשְׁמִישׁוֹ עִם הַקַּרְקַע:


מסמר השולחני – the money-changer hangs on it the scale of the balance. But our Rabbis explained that it was a fixed nail/pin set in the stand that is in front of the money-changer to put up the shutters of the shop. And the Rabbis make them pure because its use is with the ground.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 3:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:8:2)


**וְאָרוֹן שֶׁל גָּרוֹסוֹת.** הָעוֹשִׂים גְּרִיסִים שֶׁל פּוֹל בָּרֵחַיִם שֶׁלָּהֶם, יֵשׁ לָהֶן כְּמִין אָרוֹן שֶׁל עֵץ:


וארון של גרוסות – that makes pounded beans with their millstone, they have a sort of wooden cabinet.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 3:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:8:3)


**אֶבֶן הַשָּׁעוֹת.** אֶבֶן שֶׁיֵּשׁ בָּהּ קַוִּים וּרְשׁוּמִים עֲלֵיהֶם שְׁמוֹת הַשָּׁעוֹת וְתָחוּב בָּהּ מַסְמֵר וּבוֹ מְכַוְּנִים אֶת הַשָּׁעוֹת. וְקוֹרִין לוֹ בְּלַעַ"ז אירליי"ו, וּבַעֲרָבִי אלבלאט"ה:


אבן השעות – a stone which has zones and markings on them the names of the hours which is inserted in it with a nail and through it they determine the hours. And in a foreign tongue, they call it IRLIVE and in Arabic ALBALATA.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 3:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:8:4)


**רַבִּי צָדוֹק מְטַמֵּא.** אוֹתוֹ אָרוֹן, דְּתוֹרַת כְּלִי עָלָיו:


ר' צדוק מטמא – this cabinet, for it has the status of a utensil on it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 3:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:8:5)


**וַחֲכָמִים מְטַהֲרִין.** דִּכְלִי עֵץ הֶעָשׂוּי לְנַחַת הוּא וְאֵינוֹ עָשׂוּי לִטַּלְטֵל. וְרַבִּי צָדוֹק סָבַר, פְּעָמִים מְתַקְּנִים אוֹתוֹ לְטַלְטְלוֹ. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי צָדוֹק:


וחכמים מטהרין – for a wooden utensil is make to stand and it is not make to carry. But Rabbi Tzadok held, that sometimes we repair it to carry it, but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Tzadok.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 3:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:9:1)


**טֶנִי.** כְּלִי שֶׁל מַתֶּכֶת שֶׁבַּעֲלֵי בָּתִּים מְשִׂימִים בּוֹ גְּרוּטָאוֹת וְשִׁבְרֵי כְּלֵי מַתָּכוֹת. וְרַבָּן גַּמְלִיאֵל מְטַמֵּא הַכִּסּוּי, דְּסָבַר הוּא נַמִּי חָשׁוּב כְּלִי, מִשּׁוּם דְּכִסּוּי נַמִּי יֵשׁ לוֹ בֵּית קִבּוּל. וַחֲכָמִים מְטַהֲרִין, דְּסָבְרֵי כֵּיוָן דְּלֹא נַעֲשָׂה אֶלָּא לְכַסּוֹת אֵינוֹ חָשׁוּב כְּלִי:


טני – a metal utensil that householders place in it broken wares/trash and broken metal utensils. And Rabban Gamaliel defiles the cover/lid for he holds that the lid is considered and utensil, since the lid also has a receptacle. But the Sages [declare it] pure, as they hold that since it was not made other than to cover it, it is not considered a utensil.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 3:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:9:2)


**וּתְלוֹי הַמַּגְרֵדוֹת.** מַסְמְרִים שֶׁתּוֹלִים בָּהֶם הַמַּגְרֵדוֹת בְּבָתֵּי מֶרְחֲצָאוֹת, וְהַנִּכְנָסִים שָׁם כָּל אֶחָד לוֹקֵחַ מַגְרֶדֶת וּמְגָרֵד בָּהּ רַגְלָיו וְגוּפוֹ:


ותלוי המגרדות – nails that hangs on them the strigils in the bath houses and when everyone enters there, each person takes a strigil and combs/scrapes his feet and his body.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 3:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:9:3)


**וְגָלְמֵי כְלֵי מַתָּכוֹת.** שֶׁצְּרִיכִים לָשׁוּף לְגָרֵד לְהַקִּישׁ בְּקֻרְנָס, אוֹ שֶׁמְּחֻסַּר אֹגֶן אוֹ אֹזֶן, אֵינָן טְמֵאִים לְדִבְרֵי חֲכָמִים עַד שֶׁתִּגָּמֵר מְלַאכְתָּן:


וגולמי כלי מתכות – that is needed to rub/polish, comb and knock together with a mallet or something lacking a rim or a handle, they do not become defiled according to the words of the Sages until their work has been completed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 3:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:9:4)


**טַבְלָא שֶׁנֶּחְלְקָה לִשְׁנַיִם.** טַבְלָא שֶׁל חֶרֶס שֶׁיֵּשׁ בָּהּ לִבְזְבְּזִין, וְנַעֲשֵׂית שְׁתֵּי חֲתִיכוֹת שָׁווֹת שֶׁאֵין אַחַת מֵהֶן גְּדוֹלָה מֵחֲבֶרְתָּהּ, בְּהָא פְלִיגֵי רַבָּן גַּמְלִיאֵל וַחֲכָמִים. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


טבלא שנחלקה לשנים – an earthenware plate that has vertical rims/edges and it is made as two equivalent pieces where one of them is not larger than its fellow, and in this Rabban Gamaliel and the Sages dispute. But the Halakha is according to the Sages.



### Mishnah 10

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 3:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:10:1)


**אֵין טוֹמְנִין אֶת הַחַמִּין מִיּוֹם טוֹב לְשַׁבָּת.** דְּסָבְרֵי בֵּית שַׁמַּאי אֵין אוֹפִין אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן עֵרֵב בְּפַת, וְאֵין מְבַשְּׁלִין אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן עֵרֵב בְּתַבְשִׁיל, וְאֵין טוֹמְנִין אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן הָיוּ לוֹ חַמִּין טְמוּנִין מֵעֶרֶב יוֹם טוֹב. וּבֵית הִלֵּל סָבְרֵי דְּאוֹפִין וּמְבַשְּׁלִין וְטוֹמְנִין עַל עֵרוּב שֶׁל תַּבְשִׁיל בִּלְבַד:


אין טומנין את החמין מיום טובל לשבת – For the School of Shammai holds that we don’t bake other than if he created a symbolic continuity of action (i.e., Eruv) with the bread, and we don’t cook other than if he made the legal fiction with the cooked food (i.e. preparing a dish on Thursday and letting it lie over until the end of the Sabbath, by which fiction all the cooking for the Sabbath which he does on the Holy Day on Friday is merely a continuation of the preparation begun on Thursday), and we don’t keep dishes warm other than if they were kept warm from the eve of the Holy Day (Thursday). But the School of Hillel holds that we bake and cook and keep dishes warm with the legal fiction with cooked foods alone.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 3:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:10:2)


**אֵין זוֹקְפִין.** מְנוֹרָה שֶׁל חֻלְיוֹת שֶׁנִּתְפָּרְקוּ חֻלְיוֹתֶיהָ, אֵין מַחֲזִירִין אוֹתָן, דַּהֲוֵי כְּבוֹנֶה, וְיֵשׁ בִּנְיָן בְּכֵלִים. וּבֵית הִלֵּל סָבְרֵי אֵין בִּנְיָן בְּכֵלִים:


אין זוקפין – a candelabrum of pieces when its pieces can be taken apart, we don’t restore them, for that is like [the prohibition of] building, and there is building with utensils and the School of Hillel holds that there is no [prohibition of] building with utensils.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 3:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:10:3)


**גְּרִיצִין.** כִּכָּרוֹת עָבוֹת:


גריצין – thick loaves.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 3:10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:10:4)


**אֶלָּא רְקִיקִין.** כִּכָּרוֹת דַּקּוֹת. דְּסָבְרֵי בֵּית שַׁמַּאי אֵין אוֹפִין פַּת מְרֻבָּה בְּיוֹם טוֹב מִשּׁוּם טִרְחָא. וּבֵית הִלֵּל אוֹמְרִים אוֹפִין פַּת מְרֻבָּה בְּיוֹם טוֹב, שֶׁבִּזְמַן שֶׁהַפַּת מְרֻבָּה יָפֶה הִיא נֶאֱפֵית:


אלא רקיקין – thin loaves, for the School of Shammai holds that one does not bake a large batch of bread on Holy Days because of the trouble. But the School of Hillel says that one bakes a large batch of bread on the Holy Day/Yom Tov, for at the time that there is a large batch of bread, it is baked well.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 3:10:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:10:5)


**וָחֹרִי.** עִסָּה גְּדוֹלָה הַנֶּאֱפֵית עַל גַּבֵּי גֶּחָלִים. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבָּן גַּמְלִיאֵל בְּכָל מַה שֶּׁמַּחְמִיר כְּדִבְרֵי בֵּית שַׁמַּאי:


חרי – large dough that is baked on the coals. But the Halakha is not according to Rabban Gamaliel in all matters where is he stringent like the words of the School of Shammai.



### Mishnah 11

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 3:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:11:1)


**מְכַבְּדִין בֵּין הַמִּטּוֹת.** שֶׁאוֹכְלִין שָׁם. וּרְגִילִין הָיוּ לְהָסֵב עַל גַּבֵּי מִטּוֹת וְלֶאֱכֹל:


מכבדין בין המטות – where they eat there and it is customary for them to recline on the couches and eat.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 3:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:11:2)


**מֻגְמָר.** לְבוֹנָה עַל גַּבֵּי גֶּחָלִים כְּדֵי לְהָרִיחַ בָּהּ. אֲבָל לְגַמֵּר אֶת הַכֵּלִים, דִּבְרֵי הַכֹּל אָסוּר:


מוגמר – frankincense on the coals in order to smell it, but to polish the utensils, everyone says that it is prohibited.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 3:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:11:3)


**מְקֻלָּס.** כְּרָעָיו וּבְנֵי מֵעָיו תְּלוּיִין בְּצִדּוֹ חוּצָה לוֹ כְּשֶׁצּוֹלֵהוּ. וְהָיוּ עוֹשִׂים זֵכֶר לַפֶּסַח דִּכְתִיב בֵּיהּ (שמות יב) רֹאשׁוֹ עַל כְּרָעָיו וְעַל קִרְבּוֹ. מְקֻלָּס, כְּגִבּוֹר זֶה שֶׁכְּלֵי זֵינָיו עִמּוֹ. תַּרְגּוּם וְכוֹבַע נְחֹשֶׁת [שְׁמוּאֵל א יז], וְקוּלְסַא דִנְחָשָׁא:


מקולס – its legs and its bowels hanging [at its side] outside it when they roast it, and they do it in memory of the Passover offering, as it is written concerning it (Exodus 12:9): “but roasted – head, legs, and entrails -over the fire.” The animal roasted in its entirety with the entrails and legs on the head like this hero with its armaments of war with it. It is an Aramaic translation of “and a copper helmet” is helmet of copper (see Tractate Pesahim 74a where Rabbi Tarfon called it a kid with its helmet on).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 3:11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:11:4)


**וַחֲכָמִים אוֹסְרִים.** בִּשְׁלָשְׁתָּן. כִּבּוּד, מִשּׁוּם אַשְׁווֹיֵי גֻמּוֹת. וּמֻגְמָר, מִשּׁוּם דְּלָאו צֹרֶךְ לְכָל נֶפֶשׁ הוּא, שֶׁאֵינוֹ אֶלָּא לִמְעֻנָּגִים בְּיוֹתֵר אוֹ לְמִי שֶׁרֵיחוֹ רַע. גְּדִי מְקֻלָּס, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁנִּרְאֶה כְּאוֹכֵל קָדָשִׁים בַּחוּץ. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


וחכמים אוסרים – in all three [they forbid it]; serving food because of they will make holes/indentations, and the frankincense on the coals because it is not a need for every person but only for those who are most indulged or for the person who smells bad and the animal with its legs and bowels hanging [at its side] because it appears like eating Holy Things outside [the Temple] and the Halakha is according to the Sages.



### Mishnah 12

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 3:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:12:1)


**פָּרָתוֹ יוֹצְאָה בִּרְצוּעָה שֶׁבֵּין קַרְנֶיהָ.** לְנוֹי. וְאָמְרוּ חֲכָמִים מַשְּׂאוֹי הוּא וְאֵינוֹ תַּכְשִׁיט לָהּ. וְלֹא פָּרָתוֹ שֶׁל רַבִּי אֶלְעָזָר בֶּן עֲזַרְיָה הָיְתָה, אֶלָּא שֶׁל שְׁכֶנְתּוֹ, וְעַל שֶׁלֹּא מִחָה בָּהּ נִקְרֵאת עַל שְׁמוֹ:


פרתו יוצאת ברצועה בין קרניה – for beauty, and the Sages said that it is a burden and not an ornament for it. And it was not the cow of Rabbi Eleazar ben Azariah, bur of his neighbor. And because he did not protest about it, it is called by his name.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 3:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:12:2)


**וּמְקָרְדִין בְּיוֹם טוֹב.** כְּמִין מְגֵרָה קְטַנָּה שֶׁל בַּרְזֶל שֶׁשִּׁנֶּיהָ דַּקּוֹת וּמְחַכְּכִין וּמְגָרְדִין בָּהּ אֶת הַבְּהֵמָה, וְאַף עַל גַּב דַּעֲבִיד חַבּוּרָה:


ומקרדין בי"ט – it is a kind of small saw of iron whose teeth are thin. And they rub and saw with it the cattle and even though he makes a wound.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 3:12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:12:3)


**בָּרֵחַיִם שֶׁלָּהֶן.** קְטַנּוֹת הָעֲשׂוּיוֹת לְכָךְ:


ברחים שלהן – with small [millstones] it is made for that.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 3:12:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:12:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:12:4)


**מְקַרְצְפִים.** בִּמְגֵרָה שֶׁל עֵץ שֶׁשִּׁנֶּיהָ גַּסּוֹת וְאֵין עוֹשִׂין חַבּוּרָה:


מקרצפים – with a wooden saw whose teeth are thick and don’t make a wound.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 3:12:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:12:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/3:12:5)


**אַף לֹא מְקַרְצְפִים.** דְּגָזְרִינַן קִרְצוּף אַטּוּ קֵרוּד. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי אֶלְעָזָר בֶּן עֲזַרְיָה אֶלָּא בִּמְקָרְדִים אֶת הַבְּהֵמָה בִּלְבַד, מִשּׁוּם דִּבְהָא קָם לֵיהּ בְּשִׁטָּתֵיהּ דְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן דְּאָמַר דָּבָר שֶׁאֵין מִתְכַּוֵּן מֻתָּר, וְקַיְמָא לָן כְּוָתֵיהּ. וַחֲכָמִים דִּפְלִיגֵי עֲלֵיהּ סָבְרֵי כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה דְאָמַר דָּבָר שֶׁאֵין מִתְכַּוֵּן אָסוּר, וְאֵין כָּךְ הֲלָכָה:


אף לא מקרצפים – since we decree that currying with a strigil/scratching [for the sake of] currying/scraping (which wound the skin). And the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Eleazar ben Azariah. But, rub and saw the cattle only with a small saw of iron because through it we can fulfill the approach of Rabbi Shimon who stated that a thing without intention is permitted and we hold like him. But the Sages dispute him and hold like Rabbi Yehuda who stated that a thing where he doesn’t have an intention is prohibited, but the Halakha is not like this.



## Chapter 4


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:1:1)


**בֵּיצָה שֶׁנּוֹלְדָה בְיוֹם טוֹב.** בְּיוֹם טוֹב שֶׁל אַחַר שַׁבָּת עָסְקִינַן. וְטַעֲמַיְהוּ דְבֵית הִלֵּל דְּאָמְרֵי לֹא תֵּאָכֵל, מִשּׁוּם דְּכָל בֵּיצָה דְמִתְיַלְּדָא הָאִידְנָא מֵאֶתְמוֹל גָּמְרָה לַהּ, וְנִמְצֵאת שַׁבָּת מְכִינָה לְיוֹם טוֹב, וְהַתּוֹרָה אָמְרָה (שמות טז) וְהָיָה בַּיּוֹם הַשִּׁשִּׁי וְהֵכִינוּ, וּסְתָם שִׁשִּׁי חֹל הוּא, חֹל מֵכִין לְשַׁבָּת וְחֹל מֵכִין לְיוֹם טוֹב, דְּיוֹם טוֹב נַמִּי אִקְרֵי שַׁבָּת, וְאֵין יוֹם טוֹב מֵכִין לְשַׁבָּת וְלֹא שַׁבָּת מְכִינָה לְיוֹם טוֹב. וַהֲכָנָה דְבֵיצָה אַף עַל גַּב דְּבִידֵי שָׁמַיִם הִיא מִקַּרְיָא הֲכָנָה:


ביצה שנולדה ביום טוב – we are dealing with the Holy Day [that occurs] right after Shabbat, and the reason for the School of Hillel is that they say that a person should not eat it (i.e., an egg) because every egg that is born/hatched today, it was completed the day before and it is found that Shabbat is preparing for the Holy Day. And the Torah states (Exodus 16:5): “But on the sixth day, when they apportion [what they have brought in, it shall prove to be double the amount they gather each day].” But a regular “Friday” is a secular/non-holy day. One can prepare on a non-sacred day for Shabbat, and one can prepare on a non-sacred day for the Holy Day and the Holy Day is called “Shabbat,” but on a Holy Day, one cannot prepare for the Shabbat, nor can one prepare on the Sabbath for a Holy Day, and the preparation of an egg, even though it is in the hands of Heaven, is called “preparation.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:1:2)


**שְׂאֹר בְּכַזַּיִת.** לְעִנְיַן אֲכִילָה כֻלֵּי עָלְמָא לֹא פְלִיגֵי דְּזֶה וָזֶה בְּכַזַּיִת, מִדְּפָתַח הַכָּתוּב בִּשְׂאֹר, שְׂאֹר לֹא יִמָּצֵא בְּבָתֵּיכֶם (שם יב), וְסִיֵּם בְּחָמֵץ, כִּי כָּל אֹכֵל מַחְמֶצֶת, לוֹמַר לָךְ זֶהוּ שְׂאֹר זֶהוּ חָמֵץ, כָּזֶה כֵּן זֶה. כִּי פְלִיגֵי לְעִנְיַן בִּעוּר, בֵּית שַׁמַּאי סָבְרֵי מִדְּכָתַב רַחֲמָנָא לְתַרְוַיְהוּ שְׂאֹר וְחָמֵץ, שְׁמַע מִנַּהּ שִׁעוּרוֹ שֶׁל זֶה לֹא כְּשִׁעוּרוֹ שֶׁל זֶה, וְלֹא יָלְפִינַן בִּעוּר מֵאֲכִילָה. וּבֵית הִלֵּל סָבְרֵי יָלְפִינַן בִּעוּר מֵאֲכִילָה:


שאור בכזית – in regards to eating, the whole world doesn’t disagrees that both {the Schools of Hillel and Shammai) agree that the minimum [prohibited amount of leaven] is an olive’s bulk, since the Biblical verse begins with leaven (Exodus 12:19): “No leaven shall be found in your houses [for seven days]…” and it (i.e., the verse) concludes (ibid.): “For whoever eats what is leavened,” to inform you that leaven is equivalent to Hametz, just as the one, so is the other. But they dispute regarding getting rid of it. The School of Shammai holds that since the All-Merciful wrote concerning both “leaven and Hametz,” we learn from it that the measure of one is not the same as the measure of the other, and we don’t derive getting rid of it from [the prohibition of] eating of it. But the School of Hillel holds that we do derive getting rid of [Hametz] from [the prohibition of] eating of it.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:2:1)


**הַכֹּל מוֹדִים שֶׁהִיא מֻתֶּרֶת.** וְהוּא שֶׁיּוֹדֵעַ בָּעֻבָּר שֶׁכָּלוּ לוֹ חֳדָשָׁיו:


הכל מודים שהוא מותרת – for it known that the fetus completed its months.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:2:2)


**וְאֶפְרוֹחַ שֶׁיָּצָא.** הַכֹּל מוֹדִים שֶׁהוּא אָסוּר, מִשּׁוּם שֶׁרֶץ הָעוֹף:


ואפרוח שיצא – everyone admits/agrees that it is prohibited because of the moving creatures of the birds.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:2:3)


**הַשּׁוֹחֵט חַיָּה וָעוֹף.** הַבָּא לִשְׁחֹט חַיָּה וָעוֹף בְּיוֹם טוֹב, וְנִמְלַךְ בְּבֵית דִּין כֵּיצַד יַעֲשֶׂה:


השוחט חיה ועוף (this part of the Mishnah is also taught in Tractate Betzah, Chapter 1, Mishnah 2) – who comes to slaughter beasts and fowl on Holy Days and they took counsel in the Jewish court how one might do this.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:2:4)


**בֵּית שַׁמַּאי אוֹמְרִים.** בֵּית דִּין מוֹרִים לוֹ שֶׁיִּשְׁחֹט לְכַתְּחִלָּה וַיַּחְפֹּר בְּדֶקֶר נָעוּץ שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ מִבְּעוֹד יוֹם, כְּלוֹמַר שֶׁיַּעֲקֹר אוֹתוֹ מִמְּקוֹם נְעִיצָתוֹ וְיַעֲלֶה עָפָר וִיכַסֶּה בּוֹ. וּמַיְרֵי כְּשֶׁהוּא נָעוּץ בְּעָפָר תִּחוֹחַ הָרָאוּי לְכַסּוֹת, שֶׁאֵינוֹ מְחֻסַּר כְּתִישָׁה:


ב"ש אומרים – The Jewish court teaches him that he may slaughter [the animal] ab initio and dig with a mattock/pronged-tool stuck into the ground that he has while it is still daylight, that is to say, he should from the place where it is stuck and bring up dirt and cover it (i.e., the blood]. And we are speaking of that which is stuck in crushed/loose earth that is suitable for covering which is not lacking crushing/pounding.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:2:5)


**דֶּקֶר.** יָתֵד שֶׁנּוֹעָצִין בָּאָרֶץ. לְשׁוֹן וַיִּדְקֹר אֶת שְׁנֵיהֶם [בְּמִדְבָּר כה]:


דקר – a peg which we stick into the ground; the language of (Numbers 25:8): “[He followed the Israelite into the chamber] and stabbed both of them…”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:2:6)


**שֶׁאֵפֶר כִּירָה מוּכָן הוּא.** לָאו אַמִּלְּתַיְהוּ דְּבֵית שַׁמַּאי וּבֵית הִלֵּל קָאֵי, אֶלָּא מִלְּתָא בְּאַנְפֵּי נַפְשָׁהּ הִיא. וְהָכִי קָאָמַר, וְאֵפֶר כִּירָה מוּכָן הוּא, וְלֹא שָׁנוּ אֶלָּא שֶׁהֻסַּק מֵעֶרֶב יוֹם טוֹב, אֲבָל הֻסַּק בְּיוֹם טוֹב, אָסוּר, דְּלֵיכָּא לְמֵימַר מֵאֶתְמוֹל דַּעְתֵּיהּ עֲלוֹיֵהּ. וְאִם רָאוּי לִצְלוֹת בּוֹ בֵּיצָה, שֶׁעֲדַיִן הוּא רֶמֶץ חַם, מֻתָּר לְכַסּוֹת בּוֹ, אַיְדֵי דַּחֲזִי לְהַפּוֹכֵי בּוֹ לִצְלוֹת בּוֹ בֵּיצָה, שָׁקֵיל לֵיהּ נַמִּי וּמְכַסֶּה בּוֹ:


שאפר כירה מוכן הוא – it does not refer to the matters of the Schools of Shammai and Hillel but rather it is an independent matter of its own and this is how it should be understood: the ashes of the stove may be regarded as set in readiness. And it is not taught that it was heated from the Eve of the Holy Day, but if it was heated on the Holy Day, it is prohibited as we cannot say that from the day before, our mind was upon it. And if it is appropriate to roast an egg in it, for still there are hot ashes/embers, it is permitted to cover it. Since one can turn it over to roast it an egg, we can take it also and cover it.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:3:1)


**הֶפְקֵר לָעֲנִיִּים הֶפְקֵר.** מִי שֶׁהִפְקִיר לָעֲנִיִּים וְלֹא לָעֲשִׁירִים, דִּין הֶפְקֵר יֵשׁ לוֹ, וְאֵינוֹ חַיָּב בְּלֶקֶט שִׁכְחָה וּפֵאָה וְלֹא בְּמַעַשְׂרוֹת, דִּכְתִיב בְּלֶקֶט וּפֵאָה (ויקרא יט) לֶעָנִי וְלַגֵּר תַּעֲזֹב אוֹתָם, מַה תַּלְמוּד לוֹמַר תַּעֲזֹב אֹתָם, לִמֵּד עַל עֲזִיבָה אַחֶרֶת, דְּהַיְנוּ הֶפְקֵר שֶׁהִיא כְּזוֹ, מַה זּוֹ לָעֲנִיִּים וְלֹא עֲשִׁירִים, אַף הֶפְקֵר לָעֲנִיִּים:


הבקר לעניים הבקר (this Mishnah is also taught in Tractate Peah, Chapter 6, Mishnah 1)– he who renounced ownership for the poor but not for the rich, the law of renunciation applies to him, and he is not liable for gleanings, forgotten produce, the corner or the field nor for tithes, as it is written (Leviticus 19:10): ”[You shall not pick your vineyard bare, or gather the fallen fruit of your vineyard;] you shall leave them for the poor and the stranger…” What is the meaning of “you shall leave them?” It teaches about another form of leaving, that is renunciation of ownership like this, just as it is for the poor and not for the rich, so renunciation of ownership is for the poor and not for the rich.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:3:2)


**עַד שֶׁיַּפְקִיר אַף לָעֲשִׁירִים כַּשְּׁמִטָּה.** דִּכְתִיב (שמות כג) וְהַשְּׁבִיעִת תִּשְׁמְטֶנָּה וּנְטַשְׁתָּהּ, מַה תַּלְמוּד לוֹמַר וּנְטַשְׁתָּהּ, לִמֵּד עַל נְטִישָׁה אַחֶרֶת, דְּהַיְנוּ הֶפְקֵר, שֶׁהִיא כַּשְּׁבִיעִית, מַה שְּׁבִיעִית לָעֲנִיִּים וְלָעֲשִׁירִים אַף הֶפְקֵר לָעֲנִיִּים וְלָעֲשִׁירִים:


עד שיפקיר אף לעשירים כשמטה – as it is written (Exodus 23:11): “But in the seventh you shall let it rest and lie fallow…” What does it mean “and lie fallow?” It comes to teach on another form of lying fallow, that is, a renunciation of ownership which is like the Seventh year. Just as the Seventh year is for the poor and for the rich, so too, renunciation of ownership is for the poor and for the rich.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:3:3)


**כָּל עָמְרֵי הַשָּׂדֶה שֶׁל קַב קַב.** אִם הָיוּ כָּל עָמְרֵי הַשָּׂדֶה כָּל אֶחָד מֵהֶן קַב וְאֶחָד שֶׁל אַרְבַּעַת קַבִּין, וּשְׁכָחוֹ, הֲרֵי זֶה שִׁכְחָה, יָתֵר עַל כֵּן אֵינוֹ שִׁכְחָה. וְכֵן אִם הָיוּ כָּל הָעֳמָרִים שְׁנֵי קַבִּין וְאֶחָד מִשְּׁמֹנָה קַבִּין, וּשְׁכָחוֹ, הֲרֵי זֶה שִׁכְחָה, יָתֵר עַל כֵּן אֵינָהּ שִׁכְחָה:


כל עומרי השדה של קב קב – if each of all the sheaves in the field were one kab, and one was four kabim and he forgot that one, that is forgetting. More than this, it is not forgetting, and similarly, if all the sheaves were two kabs and one was eight kabs and he forgot it, that is also forgetfulness. More than this is not forgetfulness.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:4:1)


**לַגַּפָּה.** גֶּדֶר אֲבָנִים סְדוּרוֹת זוֹ עַל זוֹ בְּלֹא טִיט:


לגפה (this Mishnah is also taught in Tractate Peah, Chapter 6, Mishnah 2) – a fence of stones set up one on top of the other without plaster.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:4:2)


**וְלַכֵּלִים.** כְּלֵי הַמַּחֲרֵשָׁה:


ולכלים – the utensil of the plough


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:4:3)


**בֵּית שַׁמַּאי אוֹמְרִים אֵינוֹ שִׁכְחָה.** פְּלוּגְתַּיְהוּ דְבֵית שַׁמַּאי וּבֵית הִלֵּל בְּעֹמֶר שֶׁהֶחֱזִיק בּוֹ לְהוֹלִיכוֹ לָעִיר וּנְתָנוֹ בְּצַד הַגַּפָּה אוֹ בְּצַד הַגָּדִישׁ וּשְׁכָחוֹ שָׁם, שֶׁבֵּית שַׁמַּאי אוֹמְרִים אֵינוֹ שִׁכְחָה, שֶׁהֲרֵי זָכָה בּוֹ, וּבֵית הִלֵּל אוֹמְרִים שִׁכְחָה. פֵּרוּשׁ אַחֵר, בֵּית שַׁמַּאי אוֹמְרִים אֵינוֹ שִׁכְחָה, אֲפִלּוּ בְּעֹמֶר שֶׁלֹּא הֶחֱזִיק בּוֹ כְּלָל לֹא הָוֵי שִׁכְחָה, דְּהוֹאִיל וְהִנִּיחוֹ אֵצֶל דָּבָר הַמְסֻיָּם עָתִיד לְזָכְרוֹ. וּבֵית הִלֵּל אוֹמְרִים שִׁכְחָה, כָּל זְמַן שֶׁלֹּא הֶחֱזִיק בּוֹ. וּמוֹדִים בֵּית הִלֵּל שֶׁאִם הֶחֱזִיק בּוֹ וְאַחַר כָּךְ שְׁכָחוֹ שֶׁאֵינוֹ שִׁכְחָה:


ב"ש אומרים אינו שכחה – The dispute of the Schools of Shammai and Hillel regarding sheaves that taken possession of to be brought into the city and he placed it at the side of the wall or on the side of the stack and forgot it there. For the School of Shammai states that it is not something forgotten, for he merited it. But the School of Hillel states that it is something forgotten. Another interpretation: The School of Shammai states that it is [not] something forgotten, even sheaves that were taken possession of as such at all, it is not something forgotten, for since he placed them at some specific thing that he would in the future remember it. But the School of Hillel states that it is something forgotten all the while that he didn’t take possession of them. But the School of Hillel admits that if he took possession of them and afterwards forgot it, it is not something forgotten



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:5:1)


**כֶּרֶם רְבָעִי.** שֶׁהוּא צָרִיךְ פִּדְיוֹן אִם בָּא לְאָכְלוֹ חוּץ לִירוּשָׁלַיִם. וְהוּא הַדִּין לְכָל עֵץ מַאֲכָל:


כרם רבעי (this Mishnah is also taught in Tractate Peah, Chapter 7, Mishnah 6 and also in Tractate Ma’aser Sheni, Chapter 5, Mishnah 3) – which needs redemption if he comes to eat it outside of Jerusalem and the same law applies to all fruit-bearing trees.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:5:2)


**אֵין לוֹ חֹמֶשׁ.** דְּלֹא כָּתְבָה תּוֹרָה בּוֹ שֶׁהַבְּעָלִים מוֹסִיפִין אֶת הַחֹמֶשׁ כַּדֶּרֶךְ שֶׁכָּתְבָה בְּמַעֲשֵׂר שֵׁנִי:


אין לו חומש – For the Torah did not write concerning it that the owners add one-fifth in the matter that it wrote regarding the Second Tithe.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:5:3)


**וְאֵין לוֹ בִעוּר.** אֵינוֹ חַיָּב לְבַעֲרוֹ מִן הַבַּיִת בְּעֶרֶב פֶּסַח שֶׁל רְבִיעִית וְשֶׁל שְׁבִיעִית כְּשֶׁמְּבַעֵר הַמַּעַשְׂרוֹת כְּדִכְתִיב (דברים כו) בִּעַרְתִּי הַקֹּדֶשׁ מִן הַבַּיִת:


ואין לו ביעור – he is not liable to remove it from the house on the Eve of Passover of the fourth year and of the seventh year as one removes the tithes, as it is written (Deuteronomy 26:13): “[You shall declare before the LORD your God:] ‘I have cleared out the consecrated portion from the house, [and I have given it to the Levite, the stranger, the fatherless and the widow…].”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:5:4)


**יֶשׁ לוֹ חֹמֶשׁ וְיֶשׁ לוֹ בִעוּר.** בֵּית הִלֵּל יָלְפֵי קֹדֶשׁ קֹדֶשׁ מִמַּעֲשֵׂר, מַה מַּעֲשֵׂר יֵשׁ לוֹ חֹמֶשׁ וְיֵשׁ לוֹ בִּעוּר, אַף כֶּרֶם רְבָעִי יֵשׁ לוֹ חֹמֶשׁ וְיֵשׁ לוֹ בִּעוּר. וּבֵית שַׁמַּאי לֹא יָלְפֵי קֹדֶשׁ קֹדֶשׁ מִמַּעֲשֵׂר:


יש לו חומש ויש לו בעור – The School of Hillel derives “holy” “holy” from tithes (Leviticus 19:24: “In the fourth year all its fruit shall be set aside [literally – “holy”] for jubilation before the LORD; and Leviticus 27:30: “All tithes from the land, whether seed from the ground or fruit from the tree, are the LORD’s;] they are holy to the LORD).” Just as tithes have an added fifth and it has removal, so does the fourth year fruit of the vineyard - it has the added fifth [when redeemed] and it has removal. But the School of Shammai does not derive “holy, holy” from tithes.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:5:5)


**יֶשׁ לוֹ פֶרֶט וְיֶשׁ לוֹ עוֹלְלוֹת.** דִּכְחֻלִּין חָשְׁבֵי לֵיהּ:


יש לו פרט ויש לו עוללות – for they are considered like non-holy produce.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:5:6)


**וְהָעֲנִיִּים פּוֹדִין לְעַצְמָן.** הַפֶּרֶט וְהָעוֹלְלוֹת שֶׁלָּקְטוּ, וְאוֹכְלִין אוֹתָן בִּמְקוֹמָן, וּמַעֲלִין הַדָּמִים לִירוּשָׁלַיִם:


והעניים פודין לעצמן – The grapes fallen off during cutting and the gleaning reserved for the poor which they harvested, and they eat them in their place and bring their monetary value to Jerusalem.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:5:7)


**וּבֵית הִלֵּל אוֹמְרִים כֻּלּוֹ לַגָּת.** מִשּׁוּם דְּיָלְפֵי מִמַּעֲשֵׂר, וּסְבִירָא לְהוּ מַעֲשֵׂר שֵׁנִי מָמוֹן גָּבוֹהַּ הוּא, הִלְכָּךְ אֵין לָעֲנִיִּים חֵלֶק בּוֹ, וְדוֹרְכִים הָעוֹלְלוֹת עִם שְׁאָר הַיַּיִן, וְהַבְּעָלִים מַעֲלִין הַכֹּל לִירוּשָׁלָיִם:


וב"ה אומרים כולו לגת – because they derive it from tithes, and they hold that the Second Tithe money belongs to “On-High.” Therefore, the poor have no portion in it and they tread on the gleanings with the rest of the wine, and the owners bring it all up to Jerusalem.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:6:1)


**מְגֻלְגָּלִים.** כְּבוּשִׁים בְּמֶלַח כְּדֵי לְמַתְּקָן:


מגולגלים – picked with salt in order to sweeten them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:6:2)


**אֵינוֹ צָרִיךְ לְנַקֵּב.** הֶחָבִית, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהַמֹּהַל הַיּוֹצֵא מֵהֶן צָף עַל גַּבָּן, אֵינוֹ מַכְשִׁירָן לְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה, דְּלֹא נִיחָא לֵיהּ בְּאוֹתוֹ מֹהַל הַיּוֹצֵא, וַאֲנָן בָּעִינַן מַשְׁקֶה דְנִיחָא לֵיהּ, דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא יא) וְכִי יֻתַּן מַיִם עַל זֶרַע, כְּתִיב יִתֵּן, וְקָרִינַן יֻתַּן, מַה יִּתֵּן דְּנִיחָא לֵיהּ אַף יֻתַּן דְּנִיחָא לֵיהּ:


אינו צריך לנקב – the barrel, and even though that the thin secretion that comes out from them floats on top, it does not make them susceptible to receive defilement. And it is not satisfactory with that thin secretion that comes out, and we require a liquid that is satisfactory for it, as it is written (Leviticus 11:38): “but if water is put/YUTAN on the seed [and any part of a carcass falls upon it, it shall be unclean for you].” It is written as יתן"”/he will put on it, but we read it is as “יותן”/is put on [the seed]. Just as יתן/will put on – is satisfactory for it, so too יותן/is put on [the seed] is satisfactory for it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:6:3)


**צָרִיךְ לְנַקֵּב.** לַעֲשׂוֹת מַעֲשֶׂה לְגַלּוֹת דַּעְתֵּיהּ דְּלֹא נִיחָא לֵיהּ בְּאוֹתוֹ מֹהַל שֶׁיִּהְיֶה צָף עַל גַּבֵּי הַזֵּיתִים וְרוֹצֶה הוּא שֶׁיֵּצֵא דֶּרֶךְ נֶקֶב שֶׁעוֹשֶׂה בֶּחָבִית:


צריך לנקב – to perform an action to reveal his intention that it is not satisfactory for him that this thin secretion floats on top of the olives and he wants that it would exit through the perforation that he makes in the barrel.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:6:4)


**וּסְתָמוּהָ שְׁמָרִים שֶׁהִיא טְהוֹרָה.** שֶׁכֵּיוָן שֶׁנִּקְּבָהּ, גָּלֵי דַעְתֵּיהּ דְּלֹא נִיחָא לֵיהּ, וְשׁוּב אֵין אוֹתוֹ הַמֹּהַל מַכְשִׁירָן לְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה:


וסתמוה שמרים שהיא טהורה – for since he perforated it, he revealed his intention that it is not satisfactory to him and furthermore, that thin secretion makes them susceptible to receive defilement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:6:5)


**אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהוּא מְנַטֵּף טָהוֹר.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהַשֶּׁמֶן נוֹטֵף מִבְּשָׂרוֹ לְאַחַר שֶׁטָּבַל, טָהוֹר:


אף על פי מנטף טהור – and even though the oil drips from his skin after he has immersed [in a Mikveh], he is ritually pure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:6:6)


**כְּדֵי סִיכַת אֵבֶר קָטָן.** אִם לֹא נִשְׁאַר מִן הַשֶּׁמֶן עַל בְּשָׂרוֹ אֶלָּא כְּדֵי סִיכַת אֵבֶר קָטָן, טָהוֹר. וְיוֹתֵר מִשִּׁעוּר זֶה, טָמֵא, בִּשְׁבִיל הַשֶּׁמֶן שֶׁנִּטְמָא בְּהִטָּמְאוֹ וְנִשְׁאַר עַל בְּשָׂרוֹ וְטִמְּאָהוּ. וְהַשֶּׁמֶן שֶׁעַל בְּשָׂרוֹ לֹא טָהַר בַּמִּקְוֶה, שֶׁאֵין לְךָ מַשְׁקֶה מְקַבֵּל טָהֳרָה בַּמִּקְוֶה אֶלָּא הַמַּיִם בִּלְבַד עַל יְדֵי הַשָּׁקָה:


כדי סיכת אבר קטן – if there didn’t remain from the oil on his skin other than the anointing of a small limb, he is ritually pure. But more than this measurement, he is ritually impure, For since the oil that was defiled when he was defiled, and remained on his skin and defiled him. For the oil that is on his skin was not purified in the Mikveh, for there isn’t a liquid that receives purity in the Mikveh other than water alone through dipping of a vessel, filled with an unclean liquid, so as to make its surface level with the surface of the water into which it is dipped.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:6:7)


**בֵּית שַׁמַּאי אוֹמְרִים כְּדֵי סִיכַת אֵבֶר קָטָן טָהוֹר.** טְפֵי מֵהָכִי, טָמֵא:


ב"ש אומרים כדי סיכת אבר קטן – it is pure; more than this, it is impure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:6:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:6:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:6:8)


**מַשְׁקֶה טוֹפֵחַ וּמַטְפִּיחַ.** שֶׁיֵּשׁ לַחְלוּחִית בַּכַּף עַד שֶׁכְּשֶׁיִּגַּע בְּכַף אַחֶרֶת יִדְבַּק בָּהּ לַחְלוּחִית גַּם הִיא. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


משקה טופח [מטפיח] – that there is moisture in one pan until it reaches another pan, the moisture attaches itself also to it. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:7:1)


**בְּדִינָר וּבְשָׁוֶה דִינָר.** וּמִשְׁקַל הַדִּינָר תִּשְׁעִים וָשֵׁשׁ שְׂעֹרוֹת כֶּסֶף:


בדינר ובשוה דינר – and the weight of the Denar is ninety-six silver panicles.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:7:2)


**פְּרוּטָה.** חֲצִי שְׂעֹרָה כֶּסֶף:


פרוטה – one-half of a silver panicle.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:7:3)


**אִסָּר.** מִשְׁקָלוֹ אַרְבָּעָה גַּרְעִינֵי שְׂעֹרָה. וְנִקְרָא אִיטַלְקִי, עַל שֵׁם שֶׁהָיָה מַטְבֵּעַ הַיּוֹצֵא בְּאִיטַאלְיָה:


איסר – its weight is four globules of barley, and it is called Italki on account that it was coin that came from Italy.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:7:4)


**בְּגֵט יָשָׁן.** כְּדִמְפָרֵשׁ וְאָזֵיל, שֶׁכָּתַב לְגָרֵשׁ אֶת אִשְׁתּוֹ וְאַחַר שֶׁנִּכְתַּב הַגֵּט נִתְיַחֵד עִמָּהּ. בֵּית שַׁמַּאי סָבְרֵי, לֹא אָמְרִינַן גְּזֵרָה שֶׁמָּא יֹאמְרוּ גִּטָּהּ קוֹדֵם לִבְנָהּ, אִם יַשְׁהֶה אֶת הַגֵּט שָׁנָה אוֹ שְׁנָתַיִם בֵּין כְּתִיבָה לִנְתִינָה וְיִהְיוּ לָהּ בָּנִים מִמֶּנּוּ בְּתוֹךְ זְמַן זֶה וְאַחַר כָּךְ יְגָרְשֶׁנָּה בּוֹ, וּכְשֶׁיִּרְאוּ זְמַן הַגֵּט קוֹדֵם לְלֵידַת הַבֵּן יִהְיוּ סְבוּרִים שֶׁנָּתַן לָהּ מִשְּׁעַת כְּתִיבָה וְהָוֵי פְּגָם, שֶׁיֹּאמְרוּ מִן הַפְּנוּיָה נוֹלַד. וּפְסַק הַהֲלָכָה, לֹא יְגָרֵשׁ אָדָם אִשְׁתּוֹ בְּגֵט יָשָׁן. וְאִם גֵּרֵשׁ וְהָלַךְ הַבַּעַל לִמְדִינָה אַחֶרֶת, תִּנָּשֵׂא בּוֹ לְכַתְּחִלָּה:


בגט ישן (this section of the Mishnah is also taught in Tractate Gittin, Chapter 8, Mishnah 4) – as it is explained further on that he (i.e., the husband) that wrote to divorce his wife and after the Jewish bill of divorce was written, he engaged in sexual intimacy with her. The School of Shammai holds that we do not say that it is a decree lest people say that her Jewish bill of Divorce came before her child/son, for if we delay [the delivery of the Jewish bill of divorce] a year or two [years] between the writing and the giving and that she will have children from him during this time, and afterwards he will divorce her with it (i.e., the Jewish bill of divorce), and when they would see that the time of the Jewish bill of divorce was prior to the birth of the child, [people] would think that he gave it to her from the time of its being written, and the defect that that they would say that he (i.e., the child) was born from a freed woman. And the legal decision is that a man should not divorce his wife with an “old Jewish bill of divorce,” and if he divorced her and then the husband went to another country, she can marry him ab initio.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:7:5)


**וְלָנָה עִמּוֹ בְפֻנְדְּקִי.** וְיֵשׁ שָׁם עֵדֵי יִחוּד, וְאֵין שָׁם עֵדֵי בִּיאָה. בֵּית הִלֵּל סָבְרֵי, הֵן הֵן עֵדֵי יִחוּד הֵן הֵן עֵדֵי בִּיאָה, וְאֵין אָדָם עוֹשֶׂה בְּעִילָתוֹ בְּעִילַת זְנוּת כְּשֶׁיֵּשׁ יְכֹלֶת בְּיָדוֹ לִבְעֹל שֶׁלֹּא לְשֵׁם זְנוּת, וַהֲרֵי קִדְּשָׁהּ בְּבִיאָה. וּבֵית שַׁמַּאי סָבְרֵי, לֹא אָמְרִינַן הֵן הֵן עֵדֵי יִחוּד הֵן הֵן עֵדֵי בִּיאָה עַד שֶׁיִּרְאוּ שֶׁנִּבְעֲלָה:


ולנה עמו בפונדקי (this section of the Mishnah is also taught in Tractate Gittin, Chapter 8, Mishnah 9) – and there are witnesses of sexual intimacy there (which generally, today, follows the wedding ceremony), but there aren’t there witnesses of sexual intercourse. The School of Hillel holds that these are both the witnesses of sexual intimacy and the witnesses of sexual intercourse, for there is a presumption that nobody wants to make his intercourse of woman one of prostitution (but wants to make her his wife thereby), when he has the ability in his hand to engage in sexual intercourse not for the sake of prostitution and that he has betrothed her through sexual intercourse. But the School of Shammai holds that we don’t say that these witnesses are both providing testimony for both sexual intimacy and sexual intercourse until they have seen that she has had sexual intercourse.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:8:1)


**מַתִּירִין אֶת הַצָּרוֹת לָאַחִין.** צָרַת עֶרְוָה מַתִּירִין אוֹתָהּ לְהִתְיַבֵּם לְאָחִיו. דְּלֵית לְהוּ דְּרָשָׁא דְאִשָּׁה אֶל אֲחֹתָהּ לֹא תִקַּח לִצְרֹר דְּמַשְׁמַע מִנֵּיהּ לֹא תִקַּח לֹא הִיא וְלֹא צָרָתָהּ וְלֹא צָרַת צָרָתָהּ:


מתירין את הצרות לאחין – rivals of a woman forbidden on account of consanguinity, they (i.e., the School of Shammai) permit her to engage in a levirate marriage to the dead husband’s brother, for they lack [acceptance of] the expounding of [the Biblical verse] (Leviticus 18:18): “Do not marry a woman as a rival to her sister [and uncover her nakedness in the other’s lifetime],” that implies from this that he cannot marry neither her, nor her associate wife, nor her associate’s associate wife.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:8:2)


**חָלְצוּ.** הַצָּרוֹת מִן הָאַחִין:


חלצו – the associate wives from the brothers.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:8:3)


**בֵּית שַׁמַּאי פּוֹסְלִין.** הַצָּרוֹת: **מִן הַכְּהֻנָּה.** שֶׁחֲלִיצָתָן חֲלִיצָה:


ב"ש פוסלין – the associate wives from [marrying into] the priesthood, for their taking off of the levir’s shoe is considered a valid act of removal of the levir’s shoe.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:8:4)


**וּבֵית הִלֵּל מַכְשִׁירִין.** שֶׁחֲלִיצָתָן שֶׁלֹּא לְצֹרֶךְ הָיְתָה, וַהֲרֵי הִיא כְּחוֹלֶצֶת מִן הַנָּכְרִי:


וב"ה מכשירין – for their act of taking off of the levir’s shoe was not necessary, and it is as if she is removing the shoe of a heathen.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:8:5)


**נִתְיַבְּמוּ.** לָאַחִין:


נתיבמו – to the [dead husband’s] brothers.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:8:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:8:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:8:6)


**בֵּית שַׁמַּאי מַכְשִׁירִין.** אוֹתָם לַכֹּהֲנִים אִם נִתְאַלְמְנוּ מִיבָמֵיהֶן:


ב"ש מכשירין – them to [marry] Kohanim, if they had become widowed from their levirs.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:8:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:8:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:8:7)


**וּבֵית הִלֵּל פּוֹסְלִים.** שֶׁנִּבְעֲלוּ לְאָסוּר לָהֶן, וְהַנִּבְעֶלֶת לְאָסוּר לָהּ עֲשָׂאָהּ זוֹנָה, וְזוֹנָה אֲסוּרָה לְכֹהֵן:


וב"ה פוסלים – since they were engaged in sexual relations with someone prohibited to them, and when they engage in sexual relations with someone prohibited to them, it makes her a harlot and a harlot is prohibited to a Kohen.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:8:8
