[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:8:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:8:8)


**לֹא נִמְנְעוּ.** וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁבְּנֵי הַצָּרוֹת שֶׁנִּתְיַבְּמוּ כְּדִבְרֵי בֵּית שַׁמַּאי מַמְזֵרִים הֵם לְדִבְרֵי בֵּית הִלֵּל, שֶׁהֲרֵי בְּאִסּוּר אֵשֶׁת אָח הֵם עֲלֵיהֶם, וְאֵשֶׁת אָח בְּכָרֵת וּבְנֵי חַיָּבֵי כְּרֵתוֹת מַמְזֵרִים הֵם, אַף עַל פִּי כֵן לֹא נִמְנְעוּ בֵּית הִלֵּל מִלִּשָּׂא נָשִׁים מִבֵּית שַׁמַּאי, לְפִי שֶׁהָיוּ מוֹדִיעִים לָהֶם אוֹתָם הַבָּאוֹת מִן הַצָּרוֹת וּפוֹרְשִׁים מֵהֶם:


לא נמנעו – and even though the children of the associate wives have already engaged in levirate marriage according to the words of the School of Shammai, they are illegitimate/Mamzerim according to words of the School of Hillel, because the prohibited act of marrying one’s wife’s brother has come upon them, and marrying the wife of one’s brother [makes him] liable for extirpation and the children of those who are liable for extirpation are illegitimate/Mamzerim. And despite this, the School of Hillel was not prevented from marrying women from the School of Shammai, because they would inform them of those who come from the rival wives and separate themselves from them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:8:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:8:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:8:9)


**אֵלּוּ עַל גַּב אֵלּוּ.** מַשְׁאִילִים כְּלֵיהֶם אֵלּוּ לְאֵלּוּ:


אלו על גב אלו – they lend out their utensils one to the other.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:9:1)


**מֻפְנֶה.** שָׁרוּי בְּלֹא אִשָּׁה:


מופנה (this part of the Mishnah is found in Tractate Yevamot Chapter 3, Mishnah 5)– lives without a wife.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:9:2)


**מַאֲמָר.** קִדּוּשִׁין. וּבִיבָמָה אֵין תּוֹפְסִים אֶלָּא מִדִּבְרֵי סוֹפְרִים:


מאמר – Betrothal/Sanctification and with the widow of a brother who died without issue, does not take effect other than through the words of the Scribes.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:9:3)


**אִשְׁתּוֹ עִמּוֹ.** דְּסָבְרֵי בֵּית שַׁמַּאי בַּעֲלַת מַאֲמָר הִיא חֲשׁוּבָה כִּכְנוּסָה, וּכְשֶׁנָּפְלָה אֲחוֹתָהּ לְאַחַר כֵּן לֹא מִתַּסְּרָה מִשּׁוּם אָחוֹת זְקוּקָה:


אשתו עמו – for the School of Shammai holds that the wife the bespoken one is considered as entered into marriage, and when her sister fell [into widowhood] afterwards, she was not forbidden because of being the sister of her chained one.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:9:4)


**וְהַלָּה.** זוֹ תֵּצֵא אַף מִן הַחֲלִיצָה, מִשּׁוּם אֲחוֹת אִשָּׁה:


והלה – and this one should be go free even from the ceremony of removing the levir’s shoe if he refuses to consummate the marriage with his dead brother’s widow, because she is the wife’s sister.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:9:5)


**מוֹצִיא אִשְׁתּוֹ בְּגֵט.** דְּלֹא אַלִּים מַאֲמָר לְשַׁוְּיַהּ כִּכְנוּסָה, וְזוֹ אוֹסַרְתָּהּ מִשּׁוּם אָחוֹת זְקוּקָה. וּצְרִיכָה גֵּט מִפְּנֵי הַמַּאֲמָר דַּהֲוֵי קִדּוּשִׁין בְּמִקְצָת וְלֹא פָּקְעֵי קִדּוּשִׁין בְּלֹא גֵּט. וּצְרִיכָה חֲלִיצָה, דְּהוֹאִיל וְלֹא הָוֵי מַאֲמָר קִדּוּשִׁין גְּמוּרִין עֲדַיִן זְקוּקָה הִיא וּצְרִיכָה חֲלִיצָה לְזִקָּתָהּ. בְּרֵישָׁא יָהֵיב לָהּ גִּטָּהּ וַהֲדַר חָלֵיץ לָהּ:


מוציא אשתו בגט – for the levir’s betrothal by word of mouth is not so strong to compare it as if he is married, for this prohibits her because of she is the sister of the chained one, and she requires a Jewish bill of divorce because of the Ma’amar/the levir’s betrothal by word of mouth to his dead brother’s widow and shed also requires the ceremony of removing the levir’s shoe [when he refuses to consummate the marriage of with his dead brother’s widow], for since Ma’amar is not a complete betrothal and she is still as yet chained and requires the ceremony of removing the levir’s shoe because of the interdependence of the childless widow and her late husband’s brothers; and in the beginning, he gives her a Jewish bill of divorce and afterwards conducts the ceremony of removing the shoe of the levir [and spitting in his face].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:9:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:9:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:9:6)


**אִי לוֹ.** אוֹי לוֹ עַל אִשְׁתּוֹ שֶׁצָּרִיךְ לְהוֹצִיאָהּ בְּגֵט:


אי לו – woe is to him on his wife that he has to divorce her with a Jewish bill of divorce.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:9:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:9:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:9:7)


**וְאִי לוֹ עַל אֵשֶׁת אָחִיו.** שֶׁהוּא צָרִיךְ לַחֲלֹץ לָהּ:


ואי לו על אשת אחיו – that he has to have her [conduct the ceremony to] .remove her levir’s shoe [and spit in his face].



### Mishnah 10

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:10:1)


**הַמַּדִּיר אֶת אִשְׁתּוֹ מִתַּשְׁמִישׁ הַמִּטָּה.** כְּגוֹן שֶׁאָמַר יֵאָסֵר הֲנָאַת תַּשְׁמִישֵׁךְ עָלַי. אֲבָל הֲנָאַת תַּשְׁמִישִׁי אָסוּר עָלַיִךְ, לֹא מִתַּסְרָא, דְּהָא מְשֻׁעְבָּד לָהּ, דִּכְתִיב (שמות כא) וְעֹנָתָהּ לֹא יִגְרָע:


המדיר את אשתו מתשמיש המטה (this part of the Mishnah is also taught in Tractate Ketubot, Chapter 5, Mishnah 6) – such as the case when he (i.e., the husband) said: “your benefit of sex will be forbidden to me, but the benefit of my sex will be forbidden upon you, is not forbidden,” for he is subjugated to her, as it is written (Exodus 21:10): “[If he marries another,] he must not withhold from this one [her food, her clothing] or her conjugal rights.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:10:2)


**בֵּית שַׁמַּאי אוֹמְרִים שְׁתֵּי שַׁבָּתוֹת.** אִם הִדִּירָהּ שְׁתֵּי שַׁבָּתוֹת, תַּמְתִּין, שֶׁכֵּן מָצִינוּ בְּיוֹלֶדֶת נְקֵבָה שֶׁטְּמֵאָה שְׁבוּעַיִם:


ב"ש אומרים שתי שבתות – if he made her take a vow for two weeks, she should wait, for so we found with a woman who gave birth to a girl is ritually defiled for two weeks.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:10:3)


**וּבֵית הִלֵּל אוֹמְרִים שַׁבָּת אֶחָת.** שֶׁכֵּן מָצִינוּ בְּנִדָּה שֶׁהִיא טְמֵאָה שִׁבְעָה, וְגָמְרִינַן מִידֵי דִשְׁכִיחַ דְּהַיְנוּ הַכַּעַס שֶׁכּוֹעֵס אָדָם עַל אִשְׁתּוֹ וּמַדִּירָהּ, דְּהוּא מִידֵי דִשְׁכִיחַ, מִנִּדָּה שֶׁהוּא מִידֵי דִשְׁכִיחַ, לַאֲפוֹקֵי יוֹלֶדֶת נְקֵבָה דְלָא שְׁכִיחַ כֻּלֵּי הַאי. וּבֵית שַׁמַּאי סָבְרֵי גָמְרִינַן מִידֵי דְהוּא גָּרַם לַהּ, דְּהַיְנוּ נֶדֶר הָאִישׁ שֶׁהוּא גּוֹרֵם לָהּ לִשְׁהוֹת, מִלֵּידָה שֶׁעַל יָדוֹ בָּאָה לָהּ, לַאֲפוֹקֵי נִדָּה דְּמִמֵּילָא קָאָתֵי לָהּ. וְיוֹתֵר עַל שַׁבָּת אַחַת לְבֵית הִלֵּל אוֹ שְׁתֵּי שַׁבָּתוֹת לְבֵית שַׁמַּאי, יוֹצִיא וְיִתֵּן כְּתֻבָּה, וַאֲפִלּוּ הָיָה גַּמָּל שֶׁעוֹנָתוֹ לִשְׁלֹשִׁים יוֹם, אוֹ סַפָּן שֶׁעוֹנָתוֹ לְשִׁשָּׁה חֳדָשִׁים:


וב"ה אומרים שבת אחת – for such we have found with the menstruant woman who is ritually impure for seven days, and we learn that something that is frequent, that is, the anger that a man expresses towards his wife and makes her take a vow, and that is something that is frequent, from the case of a menstruant woman which is something that is frequent, which excludes a woman who gives birth to a female which is not all that frequent. But the School of Shammai holds that we learn a thing that he caused her, that is the vow of a man that he caused her to wait, from giving birth which through him comes upon her, to exclude the case of a menstruant woman that of itself comes upon her, and more than one week, according to the School of Hillel or two weeks, according to the School of Shammai, he should release her (i.e., divorce her) and give her a Ketubah settlement, and even a camel driver whose period is one days or a sailor whose period is six months.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:10:4)


**הַמַּפֶּלֶת לְאוֹר שְׁמֹנִים וְאֶחָד.** שֶׁיָּלְדָה נְקֵבָה, וְלֵיל שְׁמֹנִים וְאֶחָד, שֶׁהָיְתָה רְאוּיָה לְמָחָר לְהָבִיא כַּפָּרָתָהּ, הִפִּילָה:


המפלת לאור שמונים ואחד (this section of the Mishnah is also taught in Tractate Keritut, Chapter 1, Mishnah 6) – she gave birth to a female, and the night of the eighty-first day when it was appropriate for her to bring her atonement, she miscarried.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:10:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:10:5)


**בֵּית שַׁמַּאי פּוֹטְרִים מִן הַקָּרְבָּן.** מִלֵּידָה שְׁנִיָּה, אַף עַל גַּב דִּלְאַחַר מְלֹאת הוּא, הוֹאִיל וְלַיְלָה הִיא וְלֹא יָצְאָה לְשָׁעָה רְאוּיָה לְקָרְבָּן, דְּלַיְלָה מְחֻסַּר זְמַן קָרְבָּן הוּא דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא ז) בְּיוֹם צַוֹּתוֹ, הִלְכָּךְ לְעִנְיַן קָרְבָּן כְּיוֹם מְלֹאת דָּמֵי:


ב"ש פוטרים מן הקרבן – from the second birth, even though it it was after it was full, since it was at night, and the appropriate hour for the sacrifice did not end, for the night was lacking the time of the sacrifice, as it is written (Leviticus 7:38): “when He commanded that the Israelites present their offerings to the LORD…” Therefore, with regard to the sacrifice it is like the day of completion.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:10:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:10:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:10:6)


**וּבֵית הִלֵּל מְחַיְּבִין.** הוֹאִיל וּלְאַחַר מְלֹאת הוּא דְּהִפִּילָה:


וב"ה מחייבין – since it is after she completed it that she miscarried.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:10:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:10:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:10:7)


**סָדִין.** שֶׁל פִּשְׁתִּים:


סדין – of flax/linen.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:10:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:10:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:10:8)


**בֵּית שַׁמַּאי פּוֹטְרִין.** לְפִי שֶׁדֶּרֶךְ סָדִין שֶׁלּוֹבְשִׁין אוֹתוֹ בַּלַּיְלָה, וְצִיצִית שֶׁיֵּשׁ בּוֹ פְּתִיל תְּכֵלֶת שֶׁהוּא צֶמֶר צָבוּעַ בְּדַם חִלָּזוֹן הָוֵי כִּלְאַיִם בְּסָדִין שֶׁל פִּשְׁתִּים. וּבַיּוֹם, שֶׁהוּא מִצְוָתוֹ, אָתֵי עֲשֵׂה דְצִיצִית וְדָחֵי לֹא תַעֲשֶׂה דְּלֹא תִלְבַּשׁ שַׁעַטְנֵז, אֲבָל בַּלַּיְלָה, שֶׁאֵין מִצְוַת צִיצִית נוֹהֶגֶת בַּלַּיְלָה, דִּכְתִיב וּרְאִיתֶם אֹתוֹ, פְּרָט לִכְסוּת לַיְלָה, אִי לָבֵישׁ צִיצִית בַּסָּדִין, מִחַיַּב מִשּׁוּם לֹא תִלְבַּשׁ שַׁעַטְנֵז. וְסָבְרֵי בֵּית שַׁמַּאי גָּזְרִינַן סָדִין בְּצִיצִית אֲפִלּוּ בַּיּוֹם דְּלֹא מִחַיַּב, גְּזֵרָה מִשּׁוּם כְּסוּת לַיְלָה דְּמִחַיַּב מִשּׁוּם כִּלְאַיִם. וּבֵית הִלֵּל סָבְרֵי לֹא גָּזְרִינַן:


ב"ש פוטרין – because it is the manner of a [linen] sheet that we wear it at night, and Tzizit/ritual fringes which has a “thread of blue,” which is wool colored with the blood of a Chalazon/purple-shell used for dying T’kheletl, which is mixed seeds/Kilayim in a linen sheet, and during the daytime which is the time for this Mitzvah, the positive command of Tzizit overrides the negative commandment of not wearing Shaatnez/linen and wool together (Deuteronomy 22:11 – “You shall not wear cloth combining wool and linen). But at night, when the command of Tzizit/ritual fringes does not apply, as it is written (Numbers 15:39): “[That shall be your fringe;] look at it [and recall all the commandments of the LORD and observe them],” excluding clothing of the nighttime. If he wears ritual fringes/Tzizit on linen sheet, he is liable because of “You shall not wear clothing combining wool and linen,” but the School of Shammai holds that we make the decree that a linen sheet with Tzizit, even during the daytime does not make one liable, with a decree because of night-time clothing which makes it liable because of mixed seeds. And the School of Hillel holds that we do not make this decree.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:10:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:10:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:10:9)


**כַּלְכָּלַת הַשַּׁבָּת.** כַּלְכָּלָה מְלֵאָה פֵּרוֹת שֶׁיִּחֲדָהּ לְשַׁבָּת:


כלכלת שבת (this portion of the Mishnah is also taught in Tractate Ma’aserot, Chapter 4, Mishnah 2) – a basket filled with fruits that have been designated for the Sabbath.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:10:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:10:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:10:10)


**בֵּית הִלֵּל מְחַיְּבִין.** בְּמַעֲשֵׂר מִיָּד, אֲפִלּוּ קֹדֶם הַשַּׁבָּת, דְּכֵיוָן שֶׁיִּחֲדָהּ לְשַׁבָּת הֻקְבְּעָה מִיָּד. וּבֵית שַׁמַּאי סָבְרֵי דְּאֵין הַשַּׁבָּת קוֹבַעַת אֶלָּא לְאַחַר שֶׁתִּכָּנֵס:


ב"ה מחייבין – [The School of Hillel] obligates them] for tithes immediately, even before the Sabbath, since he designated it for the Sabbath, it was appointed immediately. But the School of Shammai holds that the Sabbath does not establish it until it has entered.



### Mishnah 11

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:11:1)


**וְאַחַר כָּךְ בָּא לָאָרֶץ.** שֶׁאֵין נְזִירוּת נוֹהֶגֶת אֶלָּא בָּאָרֶץ, מִשּׁוּם טֻמְאַת אֶרֶץ הָעַמִּים. וּמִי שֶׁנָּדַר נְזִירוּת בְּחוּץ לָאָרֶץ מְחַיְּבִין אוֹתוֹ לַעֲלוֹת לְאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל וְלִהְיוֹת נוֹהֵג שָׁם נְזִירוּתוֹ:


ואח"כ בא לארץ (this Mishnah is also taught in tractate Nazir, Chapter 3, Mishnayot 6 and 7) – for the Nazirite [vow] is not practiced other than in the Land [of Israel] because of the defilement of the land of the nations, and [whomever] took a Nazirite vow in the Diaspora, we require him to come up to the land of Israel to practice his Nazirite [vow].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:11:2)


**נָזִיר שְׁלֹשִׁים יוֹם.** קָנְסִינַן לֵיהּ שֶׁיְּקַיֵּם סְתָם נְזִירוּת בְּאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל, שֶׁהוּא שְׁלֹשִׁים יוֹם:


נזיר ל' יום – We fine him that he should fulfill his undefined Nazirite vow in the Land of Israel which is thirty days.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:11:3)


**נָזִיר בַּתְּחִלָּה.** צָרִיךְ לִנְהֹג בְּאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל כְּמִנְיַן הַיָּמִים שֶׁנָּדַר בַּנְּזִירוּת. וְהַיָּמִים שֶׁנָּהַג נְזִירוּת בְּחוּץ לָאָרֶץ כְּאִלּוּ לֹא נָהַג בָּהֶם נְזִירוּת כְּלָל:


נזיר בתחלה – he must practice it in the land of Israel according to the number of days that he vowed for his Nazirite vow, and the days which he practiced as a Nazirite outside the land of Israel are as if he had not practiced his Nazirite vow at all.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:11:4)


**אֵלּוּ מְעִידִין שֶׁנָּדַר שְׁתַּיִם.** שְׁתֵּי נְזִירוּת:


אלו מעידין שנדר שתים – two Nazirite vows.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:11:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:11:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:11:5)


**וְאֵלּוּ מְעִידִין שֶׁנָּדַר חָמֵשׁ.** בְּאוֹתָהּ שָׁעָה שֶׁאַתֶּם אוֹמְרִים שֶׁנָּדַר שְׁתַּיִם אָנוּ מְעִידִים שֶׁנָּדַר חָמֵשׁ נְזִירֻיּוֹת. וְהוּא אוֹמֵר שֶׁלֹּא נָדַר כְּלָל:


ואלו שנדר חמש – at the same time that you say that he took a vow for two, we testify that he took a vow for five Nazirite vows, and he states that he didn’t make a vow at all. -


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:11:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:11:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:11:6)


**נֶחְלְקָה הָעֵדוּת.** הוֹאִיל וּמַכְחִישׁוֹת זוֹ אֶת זוֹ נִתְבַּטְּלוּ דִּבְרֵיהֶם וְאֵין כָּאן עֵדוּת כְּלָל:


נחלקה העדות – since they contradict each other, their words have been voided; there is no testimony at all.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:11:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:11:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:11:7)


**וּבֵית הִלֵּל אוֹמְרִים יֵשׁ בִּכְלָל חָמֵשׁ שְׁתַּיִם.** וְיִהְיֶה נָזִיר שְׁתַּיִם:


וב"ה אומרים יש בכלל חמש שתים – and he will be a Nazirite for two [periods].



### Mishnah 12

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:12:1)


**אָדָם שֶׁהוּא נָתוּן תַּחַת הַסֶּדֶק.** אַכְסַדְרָה שֶׁנִּסְדְּקָה תִּקְרָתָהּ וְנֶחְלְקָה לִשְׁנַיִם, וְכֵלִים מִצַּד זֶה וְטֻמְאָה מִצַּד זֶה, הַכֵּלִים טְהוֹרִים, שֶׁאֲוִיר הַסֶּדֶק מַפְסִיק וְגוֹרֵם שֶׁאֵין הַטֻּמְאָה עוֹבֶרֶת לַצַּד הַשֵּׁנִי. וְאִם אָדָם נָתוּן שָׁם בָּאָרֶץ בְּרִצְפַּת הָאַכְסַדְרָה כְּנֶגֶד הַסֶּדֶק:


אדם שהוא נתון תחת הסדק (this Mishnah is also taught in Tractate Ohalot, Chapter 11, Mishnah 3) – a covered place in front of the house (excedra) whose ceiling was split and divided into two and utensils from one side and defilement from the other side. The utensils are ritually pure, as the air of [the split] interrupts and causes that the defilement doesn’t pass to the second side, and if a person was put there I in the ground on the floor of the covered place in front of the house, opposite the split.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:12:2)


**בֵּית שַׁמַּאי אוֹמְרִים אֵינוֹ מֵבִיא אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה.** שֶׁאֵין מֵבִיא אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה אֶלָּא דָּבָר שֶׁיֵּשׁ בּוֹ חָלָל טֶפַח:


ב"ש אומרים אינו מביא את הטומאה – he doesn’t bring the ritual defilement other than something that has an open space of a hand-breadth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 4:12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/4:12:3)


**וּבֵית הִלֵּל אוֹמְרִים אָדָם חָלוּל הוּא.** וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁבְּנֵי מֵעָיו בְּתוֹכוֹ, חָלָל שֶׁבְּתוֹךְ הַגּוּף חָשֵׁיב חָלָל טֶפַח:


וב"ה אמרים אדם חלול הוא – and even though his intestines are inside are inside him, the hallow that is within his body is considered as hallow one-handbreadth.



## Chapter 5


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 5:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:1:1)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר.** דַּם נְבֵלוֹת בֵּית שַׁמַּאי מְטַהֲרִין. לְגַמְרֵי, דְּלָא חָשֵׁיב כִּנְבֵלָה:


רבי יהודה אומר. דם נבילות ב"שש מטהרין – it is not considered as something that died of itself.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 5:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:1:2)


**בֵּיצַת נְבֵלָה.** כְּגוֹן שֶׁנִּתְנַבְּלָה בַּשְּׁחִיטָה. וְנִמְצְאוּ בָּהּ בֵּיצִים לְאַחַר שְׁחִיטָה:


ביצת נבלה – such as the case where it became ritually forbidden by unskillful slaughtering, and eggs were found in it after the slaughtering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 5:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:1:3)


**אִם כַּיּוֹצֵא בָהּ נִמְכֶּרֶת בַּשּׁוּק.** שֶׁקְּלִפָּתָהּ קָשָׁה וְנִגְמֶרֶת כִּשְׁאָר בֵּיצִים הַנִּמְכָּרוֹת בַּשּׁוּק:


אם כיוצא בה נמכרת בשוק – for its shell is hard, and it is completed like the rest of the eggs sold in the marketplace.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 5:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:1:4)


**וְאִם לָאו.** שֶׁאֵינָהּ נִגְמֶרֶת:


ואם לאו – and if it is not completed


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 5:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:1:5)


**אֲסוּרָה.** דְּכִבְנֵי מֵעֶיהָ הִיא חֲשׁוּבָה:


אסורה – for it is considered like its intestines.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 5:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:1:6)


**דַּם נָכְרִית בֵּית שַׁמַּאי מְטַהֲרִים.** דִּכְתִיב בְּפָרָשַׁת זָב (ויקרא טו) דַּבְּרוּ אֶל בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל, בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל מְטַמְּאִים בַּזִּיבָה וְאֵין הַנָּכְרִים מְטַמְּאִים בַּזִּיבָה, וְרַבָּנָן גָּזוּר עֲלַיְהוּ שֶׁיִּהְיוּ כְּזָבִים לְכָל דִּבְרֵיהֶן. בֵּית שַׁמַּאי סָבְרֵי, כִּי גָּזוּר רַבָּנָן בְּרֻקָּהּ וּמֵימֵי רַגְלֶיהָ שֶׁהֵן מְצוּיִין תָּדִיר, אֲבָל דַּם הַזִּיבָה שֶׁאֵין מָצוּי כָּל כָּךְ לֹא גָּזְרוּ בָּהּ רַבָּנָן, עֲבַדוּ רַבָּנָן הֶיכֵּרָא וְלֹא טִמְּאוּ דַּם זִיבָה שֶׁל נָכְרִית כְּדֵי שֶׁיֵּדְעוּ דְּטֻמְאַת הַנָּכְרִי דְּרַבָּנָן הִיא, כִּי הֵיכִי דְלָא נִשְׂרְפוּ עֲלַהּ תְּרוּמָה וְקָדָשִׁים:


דם נכרית ב"ש מטהרים (this section of the Mishnah is also taught in Tractate Niddah, Chapter 4, Mishnah 3) as it is written in the portion of a person with a flux (Leviticus 15;2): “Speak (in the plural) to the Israelite people and say to them;” the Israelite people become defiled by flux but the gentiles do not become defiled through flux. But the Rabbis decreed upon them that they would be like those with a flux for all of their words. The School of Shammai holds that when the Rabbis decreed on the spittle and urine, which are found frequently, but the blood of a woman with flux which is not found all that much, the Rabbis did not make a decree regarding it. The Rabbis made this thing recognizable, but did not defile the blood of a heathen woman with flux, in order that they would know that the defilement of a heathen is according to the Rabbis, just as the priest’s due and holy things are not burned on it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 5:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:1:7)


**וְדַם טָהֳרָה שֶׁל מְצֹרַעַת.** דַּם טֹהַר שֶׁרָאֲתָה הַמְצֹרַעַת שֶׁיָּלְדָה, לְאַחַר שִׁבְעָה לְזָכָר וּשְׁבוּעַיִם לִנְקֵבָה:


ודם טהרה של מצורעת – the pure blood that the leprous woman saw when she gave birth after seven days for a son and two weeks for a daughter.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 5:1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:1:8)


**כְּרֻקָּהּ וּכְמֵימֵי רַגְלֶיהָ.** דַּם הַנָּכְרִית מְטַמֵּא כְּרֻקָּהּ וּכְמֵימֵי רַגְלֶיהָ, דְּהַיְנוּ לַח וְלֹא יָבֵשׁ. וְזוֹ הַהֶיכֵּרָא בִּלְבַד עָשׂוּ לָהּ, שֶׁאִם הָיְתָה טֻמְאָה דְּאוֹרַיְתָא הָיָה הַדָּם מְטַמֵּא לַח וְיָבֵשׁ, עַכְשָׁיו שֶׁאֵין מְטַמֵּא אֶלָּא לַח מַכִּירִים דְּטֻמְאָה דְּרַבָּנָן הִיא:


כרוקה וכמימי רגליה – the blood of the heathen woman defiles like her spittle and her urine, that is moist, but not dry. And this is for recognition alone that they did it, for it had been defilement from the Torah, the blood would defile moist and dry. Now that it defiles only when moist, we recognize that it is defilement of the Rabbis.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 5:1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:1:9)


**בְּטוֹבָה.** שֶׁהָאוֹכֵל מַחֲזִיק טוֹבָה לְבַעַל הַפֵּרוֹת שֶׁהֶאֱכִילָהוּ מִפֵּרוֹתָיו:


בטובה – that the person who eats attaches favor to the owner of the fruits that he fed him from his produce.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 5:1:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:1:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:1:10)


**וְשֶׁלֹּא בְטוֹבָה.** שֶׁאֵינוֹ מַחֲזִיק לוֹ טוֹבָה:


ושלא בטובה – who does not attach favor to him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 5:1:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:1:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:1:11)


**אֵין אוֹכְלִים בְּטוֹבָה.** שֶׁאָסוּר לְהַחֲזִיק טוֹבָה לַבְּעָלִים, דְּרַחְמָנָא אַפְקְרִינְהוּ:


אין אוכלים בטובה – that it is forbidden to attach favor to the honors for the All- Merciful made them ownerless.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 5:1:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:1:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:1:12)


**הַחֵמֶת.** כְּמִין נֹאד שֶׁל עוֹר שֶׁנִּקָּב וּצְרָרוֹ:


החמת – like a leather skin/bottle that was pierced and tied up.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 5:1:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:1:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:1:13)


**צְרוּרָה עוֹמֶדֶת.** אֵינָהּ מְקַבֶּלֶת טֻמְאָה אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן הִיא צְרוּרָה, כְּלוֹמַר שֶׁהָיְתָה קְשׁוּרָה וְנִקְמַט הָעוֹר וְנִתְקַשָּׁה עַל הַמָּקוֹם הַצָּרוּר עַד שֶׁהוּא עוֹמֵד מֵאֵלָיו וּמַחֲזִיק מַה שֶּׁמְּשִׂימִין בְּתוֹכוֹ:


צרורה עומדת – which does not receive defilement other than if it is tied up, that is to say, that it the leather was tied and bound, and it is not stiff at the point of the tie, for since they tied it and strengthened it and it receives defilement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 5:1:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:1:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:1:14)


**אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵינָהּ צְרוּרָה.** שֶׁלֹּא נִקְמַט הָעוֹר מֵאֵלָיו וְלֹא נִתְקַשָּׁה עַל מְקוֹם הַקֶּשֶׁר, הוֹאִיל וּקְשָׁרוֹ וּמַחֲזִיק, הֲרֵי זֶה מְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה:



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 5:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:2:1)


**הָעוֹף עוֹלֶה עִם הַגְּבִינָה.** שֶׁאֵין אִסּוּרוֹ אֶלָּא מִדִּבְרֵי סוֹפְרִים:


העוף עולה עם הגבינה – (see Tractate Hullin, Chapter 8, Mishnah 1 for parallel) as its prohibition is not other than from the words of the Scribes.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 5:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:2:2)


**לֹא עוֹלֶה.** גְּזֵרָה שֶׁמָּא יַעֲלֶה גְּבִינָה עִם בְּשַׂר בְּהֵמָה בְּאִלְפָּס רוֹתֵחַ, דְּהוּא אָסוּר מִן הַתּוֹרָה, דְּהַיְנוּ מְבַשֵּׁל:


לא עולה – it was a decree lest the cheese served up [on the table] with the meat of cattle in a boiling tightly-covered pot, which is prohibited from the Torah for it is cooking.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 5:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:2:3)


**וּבֵית הִלֵּל אוֹמְרִים אֵין תּוֹרְמִין.** מִי שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ שֶׁמֶן שֶׁחַיָּב לְהַפְרִישׁ מִמֶּנּוּ תְּרוּמָה וְזֵיתִים שֶׁחַיָּב לְהַפְרִישׁ מֵהֶן תְּרוּמָה, אֵינוֹ יָכוֹל לִתְרֹם מִן הַזֵּיתִים כְּפִי הַשִּׁעוּר שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ לִתְרֹם מִן הַזֵּיתִים וּמִן הַשֶּׁמֶן וְלִפְטֹר הַשֶּׁמֶן בִּתְרוּמַת הַזֵּיתִים, דִּכְתִיב (במדבר יח) כַּדָּגָן מִן הַגֹּרֶן, מִן הַגָּמוּר עַל הַגָּמוּר וְלֹא מִמַּה שֶּׁאֵינוֹ גָּמוּר עַל הַגָּמוּר:


ובה"א אין תורמין (see Tractate Terumot, Chapter 1, Mishnah 4) - whomever has [olive] oil that he is obligated to separate from it the priest’s due and olives that is obligated to separate from it priest’s due, he is not allowed to separate the priestly gift from the olives according to the measure that he has to separate the priest’s gift from the olives and from the oil, and to exempt the oil through the priest’s gifts of the olives, for it is written (Numbers 18:27): “as with new grain from the threshing floor [or the flow from the vat],” from that which is completed on that which is completed, and not from what is not completed on that which is completed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 5:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:2:4)


**הַזּוֹרֵעַ אַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת בַּכֶּרֶם.** שֶׁהוּא הַשִּׁעוּר שֶׁצָּרִיךְ אָדָם לְהַרְחִיק הַזֶּרַע מִן הַכֶּרֶם, וְהַזּוֹרֵעַ בְּתוֹךְ שִׁעוּר זֶה קִדֵּשׁ שׁוּרָה אַחַת שֶׁל כֶּרֶם, כְּדִכְתִיב (דברים כב) פֶּן תִּקְדַּשׁ הַמְלֵאָה וְגוֹ'‏ וּתְבוּאַת הַכָּרֶם. וּסְבִירָא לְהוּ לְבֵית שַׁמַּאי דְּשׁוּרָה אַחַת קְרוּיָה כֶּרֶם. וּבֵית הִלֵּל סָבְרֵי דְּאֵין נִקְרָא כֶּרֶם פָּחוֹת מִשְּׁתֵּי שׁוּרוֹת, וְכִי קָאָמַר רַחְמָנָא דִּתְבוּאַת הַכֶּרֶם קָדְּשָׁה, שְׁתֵּי שׁוּרוֹת שֶׁל כֶּרֶם קָאָמַר:


הזורע ארבע אמות בכרם (see Tractate Kilayim, Chapter 4, Mishnah 5) – which is the measure that a person must distance the seed from the vineyard, and the individual who sows within this measure has caused the condemnation of one row of the vineyard, as it is written (Deuteronomy 22:9): “else the crop – from the seed you have sown – and the yield of the vineyard may not be used,” but te School of Shammai holds that one row is called a vineyard. And the School of Hillel holds that something is not called a vineyard if it is less than two rows, and when the All-Merciful states that the yield of a vineyard has been condemned, two rows of the vineyard are spoken of.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 5:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:2:5)


**הַמְּעִיסָה.** קֶמַח שֶׁנְּתָנוּהוּ עַל גַּבֵּי מַיִם רוֹתְחִים וּמִתְבַּשֵּׁל וּמִתְעַבֶּה שָׁם. בֵּית שַׁמַּאי פּוֹטְרִים מֵחַלָּה. וּבֵית הִלֵּל מְחַיְּבִים. וּפְסַק הֲלָכָה בָּזֶה כָּתַבְנוּ בְּרֵישׁ חַלָּה:


המעסה (this section of the Mishnah is taught in Tractate Hallah, Chapter 1, Mishnah 6) -flour upon which boiling water is poured and it boils and thickens there. The School of Shammai exempts it from Hallah and the School of Hillel obligates [separating from it] Hallah. And the legal decision regarding this we have written at the beginning of [Tractate] Hallah (i.e., if it is baked in an oven, Hallah is required to be taken; but, if any deep or covered pan and/or in pan or anything where the flame passes underneath it, is exempt from Hallah being taken).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 5:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:2:6)


**חַרְדָּלִית.** כְּמוֹ הַר דָּלִית. זֶרֶם מַיִם הַבָּא מִדָּלְיוֹ שֶׁל הַר, כְּלוֹמַר מִגֹּבַהּ הָהָר. וַאֲפִלּוּ אֵין מִתְּחִלָּתָן וְעַד סוֹפָן אֶלָּא אַרְבָּעִים סְאָה מַטְבִּילִין בָּהֶן לְבֵית שַׁמַּאי. וּבֵית הִלֵּל אוֹמְרִים אֵין מַטְבִּילִין עַד שֶׁיְּהוּ אַרְבָּעִים סְאָה בְּמָקוֹם אֶחָד, שֶׁאֵין חַרְדָּלִית מְטַהֶרֶת אֶלָּא בְאַשְׁבֹּרֶן:


חרדלית (this section of the Mishnah is also taught in Tractate Mikvaot, Chapter 5, Mishnah 6) – like HARD’LIT a stream of water that comes from the bucket of the mountain, that is to say, from the height of the mountain, and even if from their beginning until their end there is nothing other than forty Se’ah, we immerse in according to the School of Shammai. But the School of Hillel states that we don’t immerse in it until there are forty Se’ah [of water] in one place, for rain water rushing down a slope/a torrent, does not purify other than in a cavity for the reception of water.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 5:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:2:7)


**כְּפוֹרֵשׁ מִן הַקָּבֶר.** וְצָרִיךְ הַזָּאָה שְׁלִישִׁי וּשְׁבִיעִי. וְלֹא נֶחְלְקוּ בֵּית שַׁמַּאי וּבֵית הִלֵּל אֶלָּא בְּעָרֵל נָכְרִי שֶׁמָּל בְּיוֹם אַרְבָּעָה עָשָׂר, דְּבֵית הִלֵּל סָבְרֵי גְּזֵרָה שֶׁמָּא יִטַּמֵּא לְשָׁנָה הַבָּאָה וְיֹאמַר אֶשְׁתָּקַד לֹא טָהַרְתִּי מִכָּל טֻמְאָה עַד אַרְבָּעָה עָשָׂר וְטָבַלְתִּי וְאָכַלְתִּי, הַשְׁתָּא נַמִּי אֶטְבֹּל וְאֹכַל, וְלֹא יֵדַע דְּאֶשְׁתָּקַד נָכְרִי הָיָה וְלֹא מְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה, עַכְשָׁיו יִשְׂרָאֵל הוּא וּמְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה. וּבֵית שַׁמַּאי סָבְרֵי לֹא גָּזְרִינַן. אֲבָל עָרֵל יִשְׂרָאֵל, דִּבְרֵי הַכֹּל טוֹבֵל וְאוֹכֵל פִּסְחוֹ לָעֶרֶב:


כפורש מן הקבר (this section of the Mishnah is also taught in Tractate Pesahim, Chapter 8, Mishnah 8) – and he requires sprinkling [of the ashes of the Red Heifer] on the third and seventh days (having been in contact with the dead -as per Numbers 19:19). But the Schools of Shammai and Hillel did not disagree other than in the case of an uncircumcised heathen who circumcised on the fourteenth day [of Nisan], for the School of Hillel holds that it is a decree lest he become ritually impure in the next year and say that “last year, I did not purify from all the ritual defilement until [the fourteenth] and I immersed and I ate; now, also, I will immerse and eat, but he did not know that last year, when he was still a heathen, he could not receive ritual defilement; now that he is an Israelite, and he can receive ritual defilement. But the School of Shammai holds that we don’t make this decree, but according to everyone, an uncircumcised Israelite may immerse and each his Passover offering in the evening.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 5:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:3:1)


**קֹהֶלֶת אֵינוֹ מְטַמֵּא אֶת הַיָּדַיִם.** מִפְּנֵי שֶׁחָכְמָתוֹ שֶׁל שְׁלֹמֹה הִיא וְלֹא בְּרוּחַ הַקֹּדֶשׁ נֶאֶמְרָה:


קהלת אינו מטמא את הידים – because it is the wisdom of [King] Solomon, and it was not stated in the Holy Spirit.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 5:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:3:2)


**מְטַמֵּא אֶת הַיָּדָיִם.** דְּסָבְרֵי אַף קֹהֶלֶת בְּרוּחַ הַקֹּדֶשׁ נֶאֶמְרָה, הִלְכָּךְ מְטַמֵּא אֶת הַיָּדַיִם כִּשְׁאָר כִּתְבֵי הַקֹּדֶשׁ:


מטמא את הדים – Because they (i.e., the School of Hillel) hold that Kohelet/Ecclesiastes was stated in the Holy Spirit. Therefore, it imparts impurity to the hands like the rest of Holy Scripture.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 5:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:3:3)


**שֶׁעָשׂוּ מִצְוָתָן.** לְאַחַר שֶׁהִזּוּ אוֹתָם עַל הַטָּמֵא וְטִהַר בָּהֶם, אִם נָטְפוּ מִגּוּפוֹ עַל גַּבֵּי אָדָם אוֹ עַל גַּבֵּי כֵּלִים:


שעשו מצותן – After they had sprinkled them on the impure individual and he became pure through them (the ashes of the sin-offering), if they dripped from his body on to a person or on to utensils. (this section of the Mishnah, according to the commentary of Tosafot Yom Tov is not like the anonymous Mishnah taught in Tractate Parah, Chapter 12, Mishnah 4 as well as Mishnah 5).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 5:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:3:4)


**הַקֶּצַח.** זֶרַע שָׁחֹר שֶׁקּוֹרִין לוֹ נייל"ו בְּלַעַ"ז. וּרְגִילִים לָתֵת אוֹתוֹ בַּלֶּחֶם, שֶׁהָרָגִיל בּוֹ אֵינוֹ בָּא לִידֵי כְּאֵב לֵב:


הקצח – black seed that we call it NEELO in a foreign language, and it is customarily placed on bread and whomever uses this regularly does not have heart pains. [Tis is also taught in in the Tractate Uktsin, Chapter 3, Mishnah 6 and also at the end of chapter 1 of Tractate Tevul Yom, Mishnah 6).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 5:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:3:5)


**בֵּית שַׁמַּאי מְטַהֲרִין.** דְּלֹא חָשֵׁיב אֹכֶל:


ב"ש מטהרין – for it is not considered food.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 5:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:3:6)


**וּבֵית הִלֵּל מְטַמְּאִין.** כֵּיוָן דִּרְגִילִין לָתֵת אוֹתוֹ בָּאֳכָלִין חָשֵׁיב אֹכֶל:


וב"ה מטמאין – because it is customary to put it on foods and is considered food.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 5:3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:3:7)


**וְכֵן לַמַּעַשְׂרוֹת.** כְּמוֹ שֶׁנֶּחְלְקוּ בַּטֻּמְאָה כֵּן נֶחְלְקוּ לַמַּעַשְׂרוֹת, שֶׁהַמְטַמְּאוֹ טֻמְאַת אֳכָלִים מְחַיְּבוֹ בַּמַּעַשְׂרוֹת:


וכן למעשרות – just as they disputed regarding ritual defilement, they similarly disagree in the question of tithes which it defiles by the ritual impurity of foods, that one is obligated in tithing.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 5:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:4:1)


**דַּם יוֹלֶדֶת.** שֶׁשָּׁהֲתָה שָׁבוּעַ לְזָכָר וּשְׁבוּעַיִם לִנְקֵבָה וְלֹא טָבְלָה:


דם יולדת – that she delayed a week for [the birth of] a male and two weeks for female, and did not immerse [in a Mikveh].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 5:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:4:2)


**בֵּית שַׁמַּאי אוֹמְרִים כְּרֻקָּהּ וּכְמֵימֵי רַגְלֶיהָ.** שֶׁמְּטַמְּאִים לַחִים וְאֵין מְטַמְּאִים יְבֵשִׁים, אַף דָּמָהּ מְטַמֵּא לַח וְאֵינוֹ מְטַמֵּא יָבֵשׁ. וְאֵינוֹ חָשֵׁיב כְּדַם נִדָּה שֶׁמְּטַמֵּא לַח וְיָבֵשׁ:


בית שמאי אומרים כרוקה וכמימי רגליה – which ritually defile moist but do not ritually defile as dry,. Even her blood defiles moist but does not defile dry, and is not considered like the blood of a menstruant woman which defiles [both] moist and dry.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 5:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:4:3)


**וּבֵית הִלֵּל אוֹמְרִים מְטַמֵּא לַח וְיָבֵשׁ.** כָּל זְמַן שֶׁלֹּא טָבְלָה כְּדַם נִדָּה הוּא חָשֵׁיב, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהוּא בְּתוֹךְ יְמֵי טֹהַר:


ובית הלל אומרים מטמא לח ויבש – all the time that she has not immersed [in a Mikveh], it is considered like the blood of a menstruant woman, and even though it is amidst the days of her purification.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 5:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:4:4)


**וּמוֹדִים בְּיוֹלֶדֶת בְּזוֹב.** שֶׁצְּרִיכָה לִסְפֹּר שִׁבְעָה יָמִים נְקִיִּים כְּדִין כָּל שְׁאָר זָבוֹת, שֶׁאִם לֹא סָפְרָה וְלֹא טָבְלָה וְרָאֲתָה דָּם בְּתוֹךְ יְמֵי טֹהַר, שֶׁהוּא מְטַמֵּא לַח וְיָבֵשׁ, דְּדַם זִיבָה הוּא חָשֵׁיב כָּל זְמַן שֶׁלֹּא סָפְרָה וְלֹא טָבְלָה:


ומודים ביולדת בזוב – that she needs to cunt seven clean days like the law of all the rest of those with a flux, for if she did not count nor immersed [in the Mikveh], and saw blood during the days of purification, which defiles [both] moist and dry, for it is considered like the blood of someone with a flux all the time that she didn’t count or immerse [in the Mikveh].



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 5:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:5:1)


**חוֹלְצוֹת וְלֹא מִתְיַבְּמוֹת.** דְּכֵיוָן דְּתַרְוַיְהוּ זְקוּקִין לְהַאי וּלְהַאי, קַמָּא דִמְיַבֵּם פָּגַע בַּאֲחוֹת זְקוּקָתוֹ דְהַוְיָא כְּאִשְׁתּוֹ:


חולצות ולא מתיבמות – for since both of them are tied by their interdependence of a childless widow and her late husband’s brothers (i.e., they cannot marry otherwise until released from their dead husband’s brother) to this one and to that one first and if he (i.e., one of the brothers) marries the wife of a brother who died without issue, he strikes against the sister of his obligated leviratical relation who is like his wife.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 5:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:5:2)


**מִשּׁוּם בֵּית שַׁמַּאי יְקַיְּמוּ וּבֵית הִלֵּל אוֹמְרִים יוֹצִיאוּ.** בַּגְּמָרָא בִּיבָמוֹת פֶּרֶק ד'‏ אַחִין מַפְּכִינַן לַהּ, בֵּית שַׁמַּאי אוֹמְרִים יוֹצִיאוּ וּבֵית הִלֵּל אוֹמְרִים יְקַיְּמוּ. וְכֵן הֲלָכָה, שֶׁאִם קָדְמוּ וְכָנְסוּ יְקַיְּמוּ:


משום ב"ש יקיימו וב"ה [אומרים] יוציאו – in the Gemara in [Tractate] Yevamot 28a in the chapter “Four Brothers” (chapter three), they reverse it, as the School of Shammai states that they should release her, and the School of Hillel states they should uphold it [and remain wed], and such is the Halakah that if he went ahead and married [the two sisters], they may remain wed.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 5:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:6:1)


**שְׂעַר פְּקֻדָּה.** לְשׁוֹן פִּקָּדוֹן, שֶׁהִפְקִידָה הַבַּהֶרֶת אֶת הַשֵׂעָר בְּעוֹר הַבָּשָׂר וְהָלְכָה לָהּ, כְּגוֹן שֶׁהָיְתָה בּוֹ בַּהֶרֶת וּבָהּ שֵׂעָר לָבָן, הָלְכָה הַבַּהֶרֶת וְהִנִּיחָה לְשֵׂעָר לָבָן בִּמְקוֹמוֹ, וְאַחַר כָּךְ חָזְרָה הַבַּהֶרֶת. עֲקַבְיָא בֶּן מַהֲלַלְאֵל מְטַמֵּא, כֵּיוָן שֶׁנֶּהֱפַךְ שֵׂעָר לָבָן בַּבַּהֶרֶת, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁזּוֹ הַבַּהֶרֶת שֶׁהִיא עַכְשָׁיו אֵינָהּ אוֹתָהּ שֶׁהָפְכָה אוֹתוֹ לְשֵׂעָר לָבָן, טָמֵא:


שער פקודה – the language of “deposit”/thing given in charge (regarding the hair of a leprous spot which remains after the inflammation has partially receded), that the bright white spot on the skin deposited the hair in the flesh of the skin and it went away, such as the case where there was in it a bright white spot and in it was white hair, and the white spot sent away and left the white hair in its place, and afterwards, the bright white spot returned. Akavyah ben Mehalalel makes it ritually impure since the hair turned white in the great white spot even though that great white spot that is there is now is not the same that turned it to white hair, it is ritually impure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 5:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:6:2)


**וַחֲכָמִים מְטַהֲרִין.** דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא יג) וְהִיא הָפְכָה שֵׂעָר לָבָן, שֶׁהֲפָכַתּוּ הִיא וְלֹא שֶׁהֲפָכַתּוּ חֲבֶרְתָּהּ:


וחכמים מטהרין – as it is written (Leviticus 13:10): “[If the priest finds on the skin a white swelling] which has turned some hair white, [with a patch of un-discolored flesh in the swelling],” that it has turned it [white] but not its neighbor that turned it white.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 5:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:6:3)


**וְדַם הַיָּרוֹק.** עֲקַבְיָא בֶּן מַהֲלַלְאֵל מְטַמֵּא, דְּסָבַר כְּקֶרֶן כַּרְכֹּם הָיָה, שֶׁהוּא מִן הַדָּמִים הַטְּמֵאִים, אֶלָּא שֶׁלָּקָה:


ודם הירוק – Akavyah ben Mehalalel makes it ritually impure as he holds that it is of the color of the bright-colored crocus (see Tractate Niddah, Chapter 2, Mishnah 6), which is one of the ritually impure bloods but that it had smitten with leprosy.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 5:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:6:4)


**הָיָה מַתִּיר שְׂעַר בְּכוֹר בַּעַל מוּם שֶׁנָּשַׁר.** הַצֶּמֶר שֶׁנָּשַׁר מִן הַבְּכוֹר בַּעַל מוּם, הָיָה מַתִּירוֹ בַּהֲנָאָה לַכֹּהֵן:


היה מתיר שער בכור בעל מום שנשר – wool that had fallen out from the firstling which was blemished, he would permit it for the benefit of the Kohen (this Mishnah is from Tractate Bekhorot, Chapter 3, Mishnah 4).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 5:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:6:5)


**וַחֲכָמִים אוֹסְרִים.** דְּאִי שָׁרֵית לֵיהּ צֶמֶר הַנּוֹשֵׁר מֵחַיִּים, אָתֵי לְאַשְׁהוֹיֵי לַבְּכוֹר כְּדֵי שֶׁיָּשִׁיר צִמְרוֹ כָּל שָׁעָה, וְאָתוּ בָּהּ לִידֵי תַּקָּלָה שֶׁיִּגְזֹז וְיַעֲבֹד בּוֹ, וּפְסוּלֵי הַמֻּקְדָּשִׁים אֲסִירֵי בְּגִזָּה וַעֲבוֹדָה, דִּכְתִיב (דברים יב) תִּזְבַּח וְאָכַלְתָּ בָשָׂר, תִּזְבַּח וְלֹא גִּזָּה וְכוּ'. וַעֲקַבְיָא שָׁרֵי, דְּמִגּוֹ דִמְהַנְּיָא שְׁחִיטָה לַצֶּמֶר הַמְחֻבָּר בּוֹ לְהַתִּירוֹ לְאַחַר שְׁחִיטָה, מַהֲנֵי נַמִּי לַצֶּמֶר הַתָּלוּשׁ וּמֻנָּח בַּחַלּוֹן:


וחכמים אוסרים – for if he had permitted wool that falls from a living being, they would come to be compared to a firstling in order that he could remove its wool at every hour, and regarding it, they would come to a stumbling block/snare that he would shear it and work it, and those holy things that are invalid, it is prohibited for shearing and working them, as it is written (Deuteronomy 12:15): “[But whatever you desire,] you may slaughter and eat meat [in any of your settlements, [according to the blessing that the LORD your God has granted you…],” you may slaughter but not shear. But Akavyah permits it, for since slaughtering causes the benefit of the wool that is attached to it to permit it after the slaughtering, one can benefit as well from the detached wool that is placed in the window.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 5:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:6:6)


**אֵין מַשְׁקִין.** מֵי סוֹטָה:


אין משקין – the waters of the suspected adulterous woman.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 5:6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:6:7)


**לֹא אֶת הַגִּיֹּרֶת וְלֹא אֶת הַמְשֻׁחְרֶרֶת.** דִּכְתִיב (במדבר ה) לְאָלָה וְלִשְׁבֻעָה בְּתוֹךְ עַמֵּךָ, פְּרָט לְאֵלּוּ שֶׁאֵינָן בְּתוֹךְ עַמָּם:


לא את הגיורת ולא אתת המשוחררת – as it is written (Numbers 5:21): “[Here the priest shall administer the curse of adjuration to the woman, as the priest goes on to say to say to the woman – may the LORD] make you a curse and an imprecation among your people, [as the LORD causes your thigh to sag and your belly to distend],” except for those that are not among their people.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 5:6:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:6:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:6:8)


**דֻּגְמָא הִשְׁקוּהָ.** מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהָיוּ דֻּגְמָתָהּ, כְּלוֹמַר גֵּרִים כְּמוֹתָהּ, לְפִיכָךְ הִשְׁקוּהָ, וְלֹא מִן הַדִּין. פֵּרוּשׁ אַחֵר, דֻּגְמָא הִשְׁקוּהָ, עָשׂוּ דֻּגְמָא וְדִמְיוֹן וְהֶרְאוּ לָהּ כְּאִלּוּ מַשְׁקִים אוֹתָהּ מֵי הַמָּרִים, אֲבָל לֹא הִשְׁקוּהָ:


דוגמא השקוה – because they were its example, that is to say, converts like her, therefore they administered it (i.e., the bitter water) to her, and not according to the law, Another interpretation: They made her into an example, they made an example and a resemblance and showed her as if we administer the bitter waters to her, but they did [actually] do it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 5:6:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:6:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:6:9)


**וְנִדּוּהוּ.** לְפִי שֶׁזִּלְזֵל בִּכְבוֹדָן שֶׁל שְׁמַעְיָה וְאַבְטַלְיוֹן:


ונדוהו – since he neglected the honor of Shemaiah and Avtalyon (see Tractate Avot, Chapter 1, Mishnayot 10-11 and Tractate Hagigah, Chapter 2, Mishnah 2 for the listing of the “Zugot/pairs”).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 5:6:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:6:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:6:10)


**שֶׁאֵין עֲזָרָה נִנְעֶלֶת.** בְּעַרְבֵי פְּסָחִים כְּשֶׁנִּכְנָסִים לִשְׁחֹט פִּסְחֵיהֶם. דְּאָמְרִינַן נִכְנְסָה כַּת רִאשׁוֹנָה נִנְעֲלוּ דַּלְתוֹת הָעֲזָרָה וְכוּ', וְכֵן בְּכַת שְׁנִיָּה וְכֵן בְּכַת שְׁלִישִׁית, לֹא הָיָה נִמְצָא בְּכָל הָעֲזָרָה מֻכְתָּר בַּעֲנָוָה וּבְיִרְאַת חֵטְא כַּעֲקַבְיָא בֶּן מַהֲלַלְאֵל:


שאין עזרה ננעלת – [the courtyard was locked] on the Eve of Passover, when they came in to slaughter their Passover offerings, for we say, that the first division went in the doors of the courtyard were locked, and similarly, with the second division and also the third division. There was not found in all the courtyard someone distinguished in humility and fear of sin like Akavyah ben Mehalalel.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 5:6:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:6:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:6:11)


**שֶׁפִּקְפֵּק.** שֶׁזִּלְזֵל:


שפקפק – that he cast doubt/neglected


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 5:6:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:6:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:6:12)


**סוֹקְלִים אֶת אֲרוֹנוֹ.** לָאו דַּוְקָא סוֹקְלִים, אֶלָּא מַנִּיחִים אֶבֶן בִּלְבַד לְהֶכֵּר שֶׁחֲבֵרָיו הָיוּ בְּדֵלִים מִמֶּנּוּ:


סוקלים את ארונו – no exactly stone, but they place a stone alone as recognition that his colleagues were aloof from him.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 5:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:7:1)


**בְּאַרְבָּעָה דְבָרִים.** שְׂעַר פְּקֻדָּה וְדַם הַיָּרוֹק וּשְׂעַר בְּכוֹר הַתָּלוּשׁ וְהַשְׁקָאַת גִּיֹּרֶת וּמְשֻׁחְרֶרֶת:


בד' דברים – hair of a leprous spot which remains after the inflammation has partly receded (see Tractate Negaim, Chapter 5, Mishnah 3), and greenish blood and the detached hair of a firstling animal, and the handing of the bitter water to the suspected converted wife and a freed slave-woman.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 5:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/5:7:2)


**מוּטָב לְהַנִּיחַ דִּבְרֵי הַיָּחִיד.** מִשּׁוּם דְּהוּא נַמִּי מֵרַבִּים קִבֵּל וַהֲווּ דְּבָרָיו כְּדִבְרֵי רַבִּים, מִשּׁוּם הָכִי קָאָמַר מוּטָב לְהַנִּיחַ וְכוּ'. דְּאִי לָאו הָכִי מַאי מוּטָב דְּקָאָמַר, וַהֲלֹא דְּאוֹרַיְתָא הִיא, דִּכְתִיב (שמות כג) אַחֲרֵי רַבִּים לְהַטֹּת, וְחַיָּב לְהַנִּיחַ דִּבְרֵי הַיָּחִיד בְּעַל כָּרְחוֹ. אֶלָּא טַעֲמָא דְאָמַר מוּטָב וְלֹא אָמַר חַיָּב, מִשּׁוּם דְּאַף הוּא מֵרַבִּים קִבֵּל כְּדִפְרִישִׁית. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כַּעֲקַבְיָא בֶּן מַהֲלַלְאֵל בְּכָל אַרְבָּעָה דְּבָרִים הַלָּלוּ:


מוטב להניח דברי היחיד – because he also received [his tradition] from a majority, and his words are like the words of the majority, and because of this, it is stated, better to set aside, etc. and if not for this, what is “better” that is stated? Is it not from the Torah as it is written (Exodus 23:2): “you shall not give perverse testimony in a dispute so as to pervert it in favor of the mighty,” and it is obligatory to set aside the words of the individual against his will, but the reason that he (i.e., Akavyah ben Mehalalel) said “better” and he did not say, “it is better,” because that even he received [his tradition] from a majority, as has been explained, but the Halakha is not according to Akavyah ben Mehalalel in all four of these things.



## Chapter 6


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 6:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/6:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/6:1:1)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה בֶּן בָּבָא הֵעִיד.** שֶׁמְּמָאֲנִים אֶת הַקְּטַנּוֹת. כְּגוֹן שְׁנֵי אַחִים נְשׂוּאִים שְׁתֵּי אֲחָיוֹת יְתוֹמוֹת, אַחַת גְּדוֹלָה וְאַחַת קְטַנָּה, מֵת בַּעְלָהּ שֶׁל גְּדוֹלָה וְנִמְצֵאת זְקוּקָה לְיָבָם בַּעַל הַקְּטַנָּה, וְדוֹחָה זִקָּתָהּ אֶת נִשּׂוּאֵי אֲחוֹתָהּ הַקְּטַנָּה וְאוֹסַרְתָּהּ עָלָיו, שֶׁאֵין נִשּׂוּאֵי קְטַנָּה כְּלוּם, מְלַמְּדִים אֶת הַקְּטַנָּה שֶׁתְּמָאֵן בּוֹ, וּמֵאוּן עוֹקֵר נִשּׂוּאִים הָרִאשׁוֹנִים, וּמֻתָּר לְיַבֵּם הַגְּדוֹלָה. וְכֵן יֵשׁ אַחֶרֶת כַּיּוֹצֵא בְּזוֹ בְּמַסֶּכֶת יְבָמוֹת פֶּרֶק בֵּית שַׁמַּאי:


רבי יהודה בן בבא העיד. שממאנים את הקטנות – such as the case of two brothers who are married to two orphan sisters, one older and one younger. The husband of the older one died and she is now found in need of a levir, the husband of the younger, and we override her interdependence of a childless widow [the younger] and her late husband’s brother, the levirate relation, and prohibit her upon him for the marriage of the younger woman is seen as valueless. We teach the younger woman that she should refuse him for the her refusal uproots her first marriage, and then he is permitted to marry the older wife of the brother who died without issue, and similarly if there is another, in a similar manner, in Tractate Yevamot, Chapter “The House of Shammai” (Chapter 13, Mishnah 7).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 6:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/6:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/6:1:2)


**וְשֶׁמַּשִּׂיאִין אֶת הָאִשָּׁה.** שֶׁהָלַךְ בַּעְלָהּ לִמְדִינַת הַיָּם וּבָא עֵד אֶחָד בִּלְבַד וְאָמַר שֶׁמֵּת, מַשִּׂיאִין אוֹתָהּ עַל פִּיו:


ומשיאין את האשה – whose husband went to abroad and only one witnesses came and said trhat he died, we allow her to [re]marry on his word.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 6:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/6:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/6:1:3)


**וְשֶׁנִּסְקַל תַּרְנְגוֹל שֶׁהָרַג אֶת הַנֶּפֶשׁ.** שֶׁנִּקֵּר מֹחוֹ שֶׁל תִּינוֹק. וְאַף עַל פִּי דְּכִי יִגַּח שׁוֹר כְּתִיב, אֶחָד שׁוֹר וְאֶחָד כָּל בְּהֵמָה חַיָּה וָעוֹף, שֶׁכָּל מָקוֹם שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר שׁוֹר אָנוּ לְמֵדִין שׁוֹר שׁוֹר מִשַּׁבָּת, מַה לְּהָלָן הוּא הַדִּין לְכָל בְּהֵמָה חַיָּה וָעוֹף, אַף כָּאן הוּא הַדִּין לְכָל בְּהֵמָה חַיָּה וָעוֹף:


ושנסקל תרנגול שהרג את הנפש – that perorated the brain of a baby and even though it is written (Exodus 22:28): “When an ox gores [a man or a woman...],” both an ox and cattle, beasts and birds are included, for every place where it says, “ox,” we learn (Deuteronomy 5:14): “[but the seventh day is a sabbath of the LORD your God; you shall not do any work] – you, your son, or your daughter, your male or female slave, your ox or your ass, or any of your cattle…” from [the law of] Shabbat. Just as further on, the same law applies to all cattle, beasts and birds, even here the same law applies to all cattle, beasts and birds.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 6:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/6:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/6:1:4)


**וְעַל הַיַּיִן בֶּן אַרְבָּעִים יוֹם.** וּמִקַּמֵּי הָכִי פָּסוּל, דַּהֲוָה לֵיהּ יַיִן מִגִּתּוֹ:


ועל יין בן ארבעים יום – but prior to this, it is invalid, for it is wine from its vat.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 6:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/6:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/6:1:5)


**שֶׁקָּרַב בְּאַרְבַּע שָׁעוֹת.** שֶׁפַּעַם אַחַת בִּימֵי מַלְכוּת יָוָן לֹא הָיוּ לָהֶם כְּבָשִׂים לַתְּמִידִין וְנִשְׁתַּהוּ לְהַקְרִיב הַתָּמִיד, עַד שֶׁהֵאִיר הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא אֶת עֵינֵיהֶם וּמָצְאוּ שְׁנֵי טְלָאִים מְבֻקָּרִים בְּלִשְׁכַּת הַטְּלָאִים, וְהִקְרִיבוּ תָּמִיד שֶׁל שַׁחַר בְּאַרְבַּע שָׁעוֹת בַּיּוֹם:


שקרב בארבע שעות – that one time during the days of the Grecian kingdom, they did not have lambs to sacrifice the daily whole burnt offering until the Holy One, blessed be He enlightened their eyes and they found two lambs which passed examination in the chamber of the lambs, and they offered the daily whole burnt offering of the morning at the fourth hour of the day.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 6:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/6:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/6:2:1)


**עַל אֵבֶר מִן הַמֵּת.** שֶׁאֵין בּוֹ כַּזַּיִת. דְּכַזַּיִת מִן הַמֵּת לְעוֹלָם מְטַמֵּא לְדִבְרֵי הַכֹּל כַּמֵּת עַצְמוֹ. וְלֹא נֶחְלְקוּ אֶלָּא עַל אֵבֶר קָטָן שֶׁאֵין בּוֹ כַּזַּיִת:


על אבר מן המת (see also Tractate Ohalot, Chapter 1, Mishnah 7)- which lacks the equivalent of an olive’s bulk, for the equivalent of an olive’s bulk from the dead always defiles, according to the words of everyone, like the dead himself. And they did not dispute other than on a small limb which lacks in it the equivalent of an olive’s bulk.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 6:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/6:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/6:2:2)


**לֹא אָמְרוּ.** אֵבָרִים אֵין לָהֶם שִׁעוּר, אֶלָּא עַל אֵבֶר מִן הַחַי, אֲבָל אֵבֶר מִן הַמֵּת שִׁעוּרָא בָּעֵי לְטַמּוֹיֵי:


לא אמרו – limbs lack a fixed measurement, other than on the limb from a living person, but a limb from a dead person – a barley’s amount is necessary to defile.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 6:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/6:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/6:2:3)


**מְרֻבָּה טֻמְאַת הַחַיִּים.** הַשְׁתָּא פָּרֵיךְ עַל קַל וָחֹמֶר, וְהָכִי קָאָמַר, דִּין הוּא שֶׁיְּהֵא אֵבֶר מִן הַחַי מְטַמֵּא בְּכָל שֶׁהוּ אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵין אֵבֶר מִן הַמֵּת מְטַמֵּא בְּכָל שֶׁהוּא, שֶׁכֵּן מָצִינוּ שֶׁמְּרֻבָּה טֻמְאַת הַחַיִּים מִטֻּמְאַת הַמֵּתִים:


מרובה טומאת החיים – now it raises an object on a Kal V’Homer/an a fortiori inference and this is how it should be read: it is law that a limb from a living being defiles in any amount even though a limb from a dead individual does not defile in any amount, for we have found that the defilement from the living is greater than the defilement from the dead.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 6:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/6:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/6:2:4)


**שֶׁהַחַי.** דְּהַיְנוּ הַזָּב כְּשֶׁהוּא חַי:


שהחי – that is the person with a flux who is alive (see also Tractate Zavim, Chapter 4, Mishnah 6).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 6:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/6:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/6:2:5)


**עוֹשֶׂה מִשְׁכָּב וּמוֹשָׁב.** כָּל כֵּלִים שֶׁתַּחְתָּיו וַאֲפִלּוּ הֵן מֵאָה, לְטַמֵּא אָדָם לְטַמֵּא בְּגָדִים, כְּדִכְתִיב (ויקרא טו) וְאִישׁ אֲשֶׁר יִגַּע בְּמִשְׁכָּבוֹ יְכַבֵּס בְּגָדָיו:


עושה משכב ומושב – all utensils that are under it, and even if they are one hundred, defiles man and defiles clothing, as it is written (Leviticus 15:5): “Anyone who touches his bedding shall wash his wash his clothes,[bate in water, and remain impure until evening].”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 6:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/6:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/6:2:6)


**וְעַל גַּבָּיו מַדָּף.** כָּל הַכֵּלִים שֶׁעַל גַּבֵּי הַזָּב אֲפִלּוּ הֵן מֵאָה זֶה עַל גַּב זֶה טְמֵאִים טֻמְאַת מַדָּף, דְּהַיְנוּ טֻמְאָה קַלָּה, שֶׁאֵינָן מְטַמְּאִין אָדָם וְכֵלִים כְּמוֹ מִשְׁכָּב וּמוֹשָׁב שֶׁהֵן תַּחְתָּיו, אֲבָל מְטַמְּאִים אֳכָלִים וּמַשְׁקִים. וּמַדָּף הוּא לְשׁוֹן קוֹל עָלֶה נִדָּף (ויקרא כו) כְּלוֹמַר טֻמְאָה קַלָּה. פֵּרוּשׁ אַחֵר, לְשׁוֹן רֵיחוֹ נוֹדֵף, שֶׁרֵיחַ טֻמְאַת הַזָּב הוֹלֶכֶת לְמֵרָחוֹק לְטַמֵּא כָּל הַכֵּלִים שֶׁעַל גַּבָּיו אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא נָגַע בָּהֶם:


ועל גביו מדף – all utensils that are on the person with a flux, even if they are one-hundred, this one on top of that one, are all impure through indirect contact for conferring ritual impurity, that is a a light impurity, which do not defile a person or utensils like surfaces designed for lying and sitting which are below him, but do defile food-stuffs and liquids, and the word מדף/indirect contact – is based upon the expression (Leviticus 26:36): “The sound of a driven leaf [shall put them to flight,” that is a light defilement. Another explanation: the expression its breath smells, that the breath of the defilement of an individual with a flux travels great distances to defile all the utensils upon it, even though it did not come in contact with them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 6:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/6:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/6:2:7)


**מַה שֶּׁאֵין הַמֵּת מְטַמֵּא.** דְּכֵלִים שֶׁתַּחְתָּיו שֶׁל מֵת אֵין טְמֵאִים אֶלָּא רִאשׁוֹן וְשֵׁנִי וּשְׁלִישִׁי בִּלְבַד, מִדִּין מַגָּע. שֶׁהַכְּלִי שֶׁנָּגַע בַּמֵּת הֲרֵי הוּא כַּמֵּת, וְעוֹשֶׂה הַכְּלִי שֶׁנָּגַע בּוֹ אָב, וְהַשְּׁלִישִׁי שֶׁנָּגַע בּוֹ נַעֲשֶׂה רִאשׁוֹן, וְתוּ אֵינָן מְטַמְּאִין כֵּלִים, דְּאֵין כֵּלִים מְקַבְּלִים טֻמְאָה אֶלָּא מֵאַב הַטֻּמְאָה. וְכֵן כֵּלִים שֶׁעַל גַּבָּיו שֶׁל מֵת אֵינָן טְמֵאִים אֶלָּא רִאשׁוֹן שֵׁנִי וּשְׁלִישִׁי בִּלְבַד מִדִּין מַגָּע, וְלֹא מִדִּין מִשְׁכָּב וּמוֹשָׁב:


מה שאין המת מטמא – for the utensils that are under the dead person are not defiled other than first-degree and second-degree and third-degree alone. From the law of contact, for the utensils that came in contact/touched the dead is like the dead, and makes the utensil that it came in contact with a primary [level of defilement], and [something that is is] third level of defilement that came in contact with it is made into first-degree [of defilement] and further than that, does not defile utensils. For utensils do not receive defilement other than from a primary-level of defilement. And similarly, utensils that are upon the dead do not defile other than first-degree, second-degree or third degree alone, from the law of contact, and not from the law of lying and sitting.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 6:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/6:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/6:3:1)


**כַּזַּיִת בָּשָׂר הַפּוֹרֵשׁ מֵאֵבָר מִן הַחַי.** אֵבֶר שֶׁפֵּרַשׁ מִן הָאָדָם הַחַי, דִּינוֹ שֶׁהוּא מְטַמֵּא כָּל זְמַן שֶׁהוּא אֵבֶר שָׁלֵם, בְּמַגָּע וּבְמַשָּׂא וּבְאֹהֶל, כַּמֵּת עַצְמוֹ, דִּכְתִיב (במדבר יט) בַּחֲלַל חֶרֶב אוֹ בְּמֵת, אֵבֶר שֶׁהִבְדִּילַתּוּ הַחֶרֶב מִן הַחַי הֲרֵי הוּא כַּמֵּת. וְהַבָּשָׂר הַפּוֹרֵשׁ מִן הַחַי אֵינוֹ מְטַמֵּא עַד שֶׁיִּהְיֶה אֵבֶר שָׁלֵם. וּכְשֶׁפֵּרַשׁ כַּזַּיִת בָּשָׂר מֵאֵבָר מִן הַחַי, רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר מְטַמֵּא כְּדִמְפָרֵשׁ טַעֲמָא וְאָזֵיל:


כזית בשר הפורש מאבר מן החי – a limb that separates from a living person, it’s law is that it defiles all the while it is a complete limb while in contact and while being carried and in the tent of the dead person himself, as it is written (Numbers 19:16): “[And in the open, anyone who touches a person] who was killed, or who died naturally...,” the limb that had been severed by the sword from the living, it is like the dead. And the flesh that separates from the living does not defile until it becomes a complete limb and when an olive’s bulk of flesh separates from the limb of a living person, Rabbi Eliezer defiles as it explains the reason further on.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 6:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/6:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/6:3:2)


**עֶצֶם כַּשְּׂעֹרָה.** מִן הַמֵּת, מְטַמֵּא בְּמַגָּע וּבְמַשָּׂא וְאֵינוֹ מְטַמֵּא בְּאֹהֶל, דִּכְתִיב (שם) וְעַל הַנֹּגֵעַ בַּעֶצֶם, וַהֲלָכָה לְמֹשֶׁה מִסִּינַי שֶׁהוּא מְטַמֵּא בְּכַשְּׂעֹרָה. וּכְשֶׁפֵּרַשׁ מֵאֵבָר מִן הַחַי רַבִּי נְחוּנְיָא מְטַמֵּא וְכוּ':


עצם כשעורה – from the dead defiles through contact and through carrying but it does not defile in the tent, as it is written (Numbers 19:18): ‘”or on him who touched the bones [or the person who was killed or died naturally or the grave],” and it is a traditional interpretation of a written law that it defiles with a barley seed in bulk, and when it separates from a living limb, Rabbi Nehuniah defiles it, etc.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 6:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/6:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/6:3:3)


**מָצִינוּ אֵבֶר מִן הַחַי כְּמֵת שָׁלֵם.** כִּדְיָלְפִינַן מִקְרָא בַּחֲלַל חֶרֶב אוֹ בְּמֵת:


מצינו אבר מן החי כמת עצמו – that we derive it from the Biblical verse (Numbers 19:16): “who was killed or died naturally.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 6:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/6:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/6:3:4)


**אִם טִמֵּאתָ כַּזַּיִת בָּשָׂר הַפּוֹרֵשׁ מִן הַמֵּת.** כְּלוֹמַר דִּין הוּא שֶׁכַּזַּיִת בָּשָׂר הַפּוֹרֵשׁ מִן הַמֵּת יְהֵא טָמֵא, שֶׁכֵּן יֵשׁ בּוֹ חֻמְרָא אַחֶרֶת, שֶׁעֶצֶם כַּשְּׂעֹרָה הַפּוֹרֵשׁ מִמֶּנּוּ נַמִּי טָמֵא:


אם טמאת כזית בשר הפורש מן המת – that is to say, it is a law that an olive’s bulk of flesh that separates from the dead person will be defiled, just as it has another stringency that the bone is like a barley-seed in bulk that separates from it is also impure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 6:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/6:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/6:3:5)


**אֲבָל נְטַמֵּא כַּזַּיִת בָּשָׂר הַפּוֹרֵשׁ מֵאֵבָר מִן הַחַי.** בִּתְמִיָּה. שֶׁהֲרֵי אֵין בּוֹ זֹאת הַחֻמְרָא:


אבל נטמא כזית בשר הפורש מאבר מן החי – in astonishment, for there isn’t in this that stringency.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 6:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/6:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/6:3:6)


**שֶׁכֵּן טִהַרְתָּ עֶצֶם כַּשְּׂעֹרָה הַפּוֹרֵשׁ מִמֶּנּוּ.** דְּהָא תְּנַן לְעֵיל עֶצֶם כַּשְּׂעֹרָה הַפּוֹרֵשׁ מֵאֵבָר מִן הַחַי רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר וְרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ מְטַהֲרִין. וּמִדִּבְרֵי עַצְמוֹ עָשׂוּ פִּרְכָא לְמַה מָּצִינוּ שֶׁהֵבִיא, וְכֵן לְרַבִּי נְחוּנְיָא:


שכן טהרת עצם כשעורה הפורש ממנו – for it is taught in the Mishnah above that a bone which is a barley-seed in bulk that separates from a living limb, Rabbi Eliezer and RabbiYehoshua declare if pure, but from its own words, they made an objection/refutation what did we find that he brought and similarly to Rabbi Nehunia..”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 6:3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/6:3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/6:3:7)


**שֶׁהַבָּשָׂר נוֹהֵג בַּנְּבֵלוֹת וּבַשְּׁרָצִים וְכוּ'.** דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא יא) וְהַנֹּגֵעַ בְּנִבְלָתָם, בְּנִבְלָתָם וְלֹא בָּעֲצָמוֹת וְלֹא בַּקַּרְנַיִם וְלֹא בַּטְּלָפַיִם, אַלְמָא אֵין הָעֲצָמוֹת מְטַמְּאִים מִשּׁוּם נְבֵלָה:


שהבשר נוהג בנבילות ובשרצים וכו' – as it is written (Leviticus 11:36): “[However, a spring or cistern in which water is collected shall be pure,] but whoever touches such a carcass in it shall be impure.” Such a carcass, and not the bones, and not the horns and not the cloven hoofs. So we see that the bones do not defile because of a carrion (that dies of itself).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 6:3:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/6:3:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/6:3:8)


**אֵבֶר שֶׁיֵּשׁ עָלָיו בָּשָׂר כָּרָאוּי.** אֵבֶר אֵינוֹ מְטַמֵּא אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן יֵשׁ בּוֹ בָּשָׂר וְגִידִים וַעֲצָמוֹת, דִּכְתִיב (במדבר יט) אוֹ בְּעֶצֶם אָדָם, מָה אָדָם שֶׁיֵּשׁ בּוֹ בָּשָׂר וְגִידִים וַעֲצָמוֹת, אַף כָּל שֶׁיֵּשׁ בּוֹ בָּשָׂר וְגִידִים וַעֲצָמוֹת. וְאִם חָסֵר מִן הַבָּשָׂר שֶׁהָיָה עָלָיו וְנִשְׁאַר בּוֹ בָּשָׂר כָּרָאוּי שֶׁיַּעֲלֶה אֲרוּכָה וְיַבְרִיא אִם הָיָה מְחֻבָּר בְּאָדָם חַי, מְטַמֵּא מִשּׁוּם אֵבֶר. וְהַיְנוּ דְּקָאָמַר חָסֵר הַבָּשָׂר טָמֵא. אֲבָל אִם נֶחְסַר כָּל שֶׁהוּא מִן הָעֶצֶם שֶׁבָּאֵבָר, שׁוּב אֵינוֹ מְטַמֵּא מִשּׁוּם אֵבֶר. וְהַיְנוּ דְּקָאָמַר חָסֵר הָעֶצֶם טָהוֹר, כְּלוֹמַר טָהוֹר מִשּׁוּם אֵבֶר אֲבָל טָמֵא מִשּׁוּם בָּשָׂר, וְאִם כֵּן מָצִינוּ שֶׁמְּרֻבָּה טֻמְאַת בָּשָׂר מִטֻּמְאַת עֶצֶם:


אבר שיש עליו בשר כראוי – a limb does not defile other than if it has flesh and sinews and bones, as it is written (Numbers 19:16)” “or human bone,” just as a person who has flesh and sinews and bones, also all that have flesh and sinews and bones, and if it is missing from the flesh that was upon it and there remains upon it flesh as appropriate that will produce new flesh on a healing wound if it is attached to a living person, it defiles because of the limb, and that is - as we have said that if is missing the flesh, it is impure, but if it is missing a little bit from the bone in the limb, it does not defile because of the limb and that is as we have said, missing the bone, it is pure. That is to say, pure because of the limb, but it is impure because of the flesh and if so, we found that the defilement of the flesh is greater than the defilement from the bone.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 6:3:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/6:3:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/6:3:9)


**וְהוּא כִּבְרִיָתוֹ.** בָּשָׂר וְגִידִים וַעֲצָמוֹת:


והוא כברייתו – flesh and sinews and bones.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 6:3:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/6:3:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/6:3:10)


**כַּזַּיִת בָּשָׂר מְטַמֵּא בְמַגָּע וּבְמַשָּׂא וּבְאֹהֶל.** אָמְרוּ לְפִי שֶׁתְּחִלַּת בְּרִיָּתוֹ שֶׁל אָדָם כַּזַּיִת, לְפִיכָךְ שִׁעוּר טֻמְאָתוֹ בְּכַזַּיִת:


כזית בשר מטמא במגע ובמשא ובאהל – they said according to the beginning of the creation of man is as an olive’s bulk. Therefore the measure of his defilement is an olive’s bulk.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 6:3:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/6:3:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/6:3:11)


**וְרֹב עֲצָמוֹת.** רֹב מִנְיַן עַצְמוֹתָיו שֶׁל אָדָם, שֶׁמִּנְיַן עַצְמוֹתָיו שֶׁל אָדָם מָאתַיִם אַרְבָּעִים וּשְׁמֹנֶה, נִמְצָא הָרֹב מֵאָה וְעֶשְׂרִים וְחָמֵשׁ:


ורוב עצמות – the majority of the number of the bones of a human being, since the number of the bones of a human is two-hundred and forty-eight. It is found that the majority is one-hundred and twenty-five.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 6:3:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/6:3:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/6:3:12)


**חָסֵר הַבָּשָׂר.** מִשִּׁעוּר שֶׁל כַּזַּיִת:


חסר הבשר – from the measurement of an olive’s bulk


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 6:3:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/6:3:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/6:3:13)


**טָהוֹר.** גָּמוּר מִלְּטַמֵּא, לֹא בְּמַגָּע וְלֹא בְּמַשָּׂא וְלֹא בְּאֹהֶל:


טהור – completely from being defiled not through contact and not through carrying nor in a tent.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 6:3:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/6:3:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/6:3:14)


**חָסֵר רֹב עֲצָמוֹת מְטַמֵּא בְמַגָּע וּבְמַשָּׂא.** דְּעֶצֶם כַּשְּׂעֹרָה מְטַמֵּא בְּמַגָּע וּבְמַשָּׂא וְאֵינוֹ מְטַמֵּא בְּאֹהֶל, אַלְמָא מְרֻבָּה טֻמְאַת עֲצָמוֹת מִטֻּמְאַת בָּשָׂר, דְּאִלּוּ עֲצָמוֹת כְּשֶׁנֶּחְסְרוּ מִשִּׁעוּרָן עֲדַיִן נִשְׁאַר בָּהֶן טֻמְאָה, וּבָשָׂר כְּשֶׁנֶּחְסַר מִשִּׁעוּרוֹ טָהוֹר גָּמוּר:


חסר רוב עצמות מטמא במגע ובמשא – for a bone like a barley-seed in bulk defiles through contact and through carrying, but does not defile in the tent. So we see that the defilement of bones is greater than defilement by flesh, for if it were the bones that were missing from their measurement, still defilement would remain in them, but flesh that is missing from its measure is completely pure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 6:3:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/6:3:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/6:3:15)


**רֹב בִּנְיָנוֹ.** כְּגוֹן שְׁתֵּי שׁוֹקַיִם וְיָרֵךְ אֶחָד. וְכָל בִּנְיָנוֹ שֶׁל אָדָם הֵן שְׁתֵּי שׁוֹקַיִם וְהַיְרֵכַיִם וְהַצְּלָעוֹת וְהַשִּׁדְרָה:


רוב בנינו – such as two lower legs and one thigh and all of the skeleton of a person are two lower legs and the thighs and the ribs and the backbone.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 6:3:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/6:3:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/6:3:16)


**אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵין בָּהֶן רֹבַע.** דְּרֹבַע קַב עֲצָמוֹת שֶׁל מֵת מְטַמְּאִים בְּאֹהֶל אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵין בָּהֶם רֹב מִנְיָן וְלֹא רֹב בִּנְיָן. וְכֵן רֹב מִנְיָן אוֹ רֹב בִּנְיָן מְטַמְּאִים אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵין בָּהֶן רֹבַע. אֲבָל בָּשָׂר כְּשֶׁהוּא פָּחוּת מִכַּזַּיִת אֵין לְךָ בּוֹ דָּבָר שֶׁיָּבִיא לוֹ אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה:


אע"פ שאין בהן רובע – for one-quarter kab of ones of a dead person defile in the tent. Even though they don’t have the majority of the bones [of a human being] or the majority of the skeleton, they defile even though they lack the one-quarter [kab]. But flesh which is less than the equivalent of an olive’s bulk, you have nothing in it that will bring to him the defilement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 6:3:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/6:3:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/6:3:17)


**אִם אֲמַרְתֶּם בַּמֵּת שֶׁיֶּשׁ בּוֹ רֹב וְרֹבַע וְרָקָב.** דִּין הוּא שֶׁכַּזַּיִת בָּשָׂר וְעֶצֶם כַּשְּׂעֹרָה הַפּוֹרְשִׁים מִן הַמֵּת שֶׁיִּהְיוּ טְמֵאִים, שֶׁכֵּן יֵשׁ בַּמֵּת חֻמְרוֹת שֶׁל רֹב וְרֹבַע וְרָקָב:


אם אמרתם במת שיש בו רוב ורובע ורקב – it is the law that the equivalent of an olive’s bulk of flesh and a barley-side’s bulk of a bone that separates from the dead will be impure, for there are stringencies regarding the dead of a majority and one-quarter [kab] and a mass of earth from a grave containing parts of a decayed human body.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 6:3:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/6:3:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/6:3:18)


**תֹּאמְרוּ בַחַי שֶׁאֵין בּוֹ.** חֻמְרוֹת הַלָּלוּ, שֶׁאֵינוֹ דִּין שֶׁיִּהְיוּ לֹא כַּזַּיִת בָּשָׂר וְלֹא עֶצֶם כַּשְּׂעֹרָה הַפּוֹרְשִׁים מֵאֵבָר מִן הַחַי טְמֵאִים אֶלָּא טְהוֹרִים. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ:


תאמרו בחי שאין בו – these stringencies which is not the law that there would be neither the equivalent of an olive’s bulk of flesh nor a barley-seed’s equivalent of a bone that separate from the limb of a living person, they are [not] impure, but rather pure. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yehoshua.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 6:3:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/6:3:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/6:3:19)


**וְרָקָב.** גּוּפוֹ שֶׁל מֵת כְּשֶׁכָּלָה לַחוּתוֹ וְנַעֲשָׂה כְּמִין עָפָר, זֶהוּ רָקָב, שֶׁמְּטַמֵּא כִּמְלֹא תַּרְוָד, וְשִׁעוּרוֹ מְלֹא חָפְנַיִם שֶׁל אָדָם בֵּינוֹנִי. וְאֵין מְלֹא תַּרְוַד רָקָב מְטַמֵּא אֶלָּא מִן הַמֵּת שֶׁנִּקְבַּר עָרֹם בְּאָרוֹן שֶׁל שַׁיִשׁ וּמְכֻסֶּה בְּכִסּוּי שֶׁל שַׁיִשׁ, עַד שֶׁנּוֹדָע בְּוַדַּאי שֶׁאֵין בּוֹ תַּעֲרֹבֶת רִקָּבוֹן שֶׁל בֶּגֶד אוֹ שֶׁל עֵץ אוֹ עָפָר אַחֵר. אֲבָל מֵת שֶׁנִּקְבַּר בִּכְסוּתוֹ אוֹ בְּאָרוֹן שֶׁל עֵץ אוֹ בֶּעָפָר, אֵין לוֹ רָקָב. וְכֵן מֵת שֶׁנִּקְבַּר חֲסַר אֵבֶר אֵין לוֹ רָקָב:


ורקב – the body of a dead whose moistness had ceased, and had become like dirt, this is רקב/decay of the body that defiles a spoon-filled of dust/tarvad-full of dust [from parts of a decayed human body] and its measurement is the handful of an intermediate-size individual. But a handful of a decayed human body does not defile other than from a dead person buried naked in an alabaster coffin which is covered with a marble cover until it is definitively known that there is no mixture of a decay of clothing or of wood or other dirt. But if the dead is buried in his clothing or in a wooden coffin or in the dust, there is no decay, and similarly, a dead person that is buried missing a limb, there is no decay.



## Chapter 7


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 7:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/7:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/7:1:1)


**הֵעִיד רַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ.** פִּדְיוֹן פֶּטֶר חֲמוֹר שֶׁמֵּת. שֶׂה שֶׁהִפְרִישׁוֹ לְפִדְיוֹן פֶּטֶר חֲמוֹר וּמֵת:


העיד רבי יהושע. פדיון פטר חמור שמת– a lamb which was set aside/dedicated for the redemption of the first-born of an ass and it died.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 7:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/7:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/7:1:2)


**כְּחָמֵשׁ סְלָעִים.** שֶׁל פִּדְיוֹן הַבֵּן שֶׁחַיָּב בְּאַחֲרָיוּתָן:


כחמש סלעים – [like the five Sela’im] of the redemption of the first-born son [if lost] is liable to make it up.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eduyot 7:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/7:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eduyot/r/7:1:3)


**כְּפִדְיוֹן מַעֲשֵׂר שֵׁנִי.** דְּאִם אָבַד אֵינוֹ חַיָּב בְּאַחֲרָיוּתוֹ, דְּהַהוּא כֶּסֶף חַיְּבֵיהּ רַחְמָנָא לֶאֱכֹל בִּירוּשָׁלַיִם, וְהָא אָזַל לֵיהּ. וְטַעֲמָא דְרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר, שֶׁכֵּן מָצִינוּ שֶׁהֵקִישׁ הַכָּתוּב פֶּטֶר חֲמוֹר לִבְכוֹר אָדָם, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (שמות לד) וּפֶטֶר חֲמוֹר תִּפְדֶּה בְשֶׂה וְגוֹ'‏ וְכֹל בְּכוֹר בָּנֶיךָ תִּפְדֶּה:
